# Ouroboros v6.119.2 — Architecture & Reference

This file is NOT a changelog. Version history lives in README.md, git tags, and commit log.

This document is the current operational map of Ouroboros: structure, data flows, APIs, protected boundaries, and the rationale for non-obvious architectural choices. Rationale must be self-contained here; future maintainers should not need to open old commits to understand why a guard, review gate, or lifecycle exists.

---

## 1. High-Level Architecture

```
User
  │
  ▼
launcher.py (PyWebView)       ← desktop window, release-reviewed outer shell (source in git; running packaged copy outside managed hot-swap)
  │
  │  spawns subprocess
  ▼
server.py (Starlette+uvicorn) ← HTTP + WebSocket on configurable host:port (default localhost:8765; Docker/non-loopback via OUROBOROS_SERVER_HOST=0.0.0.0)
  │
  ├── web/ ← Web UI (SPA with ES modules in web/modules/)
  │   └── modules/review_presentation.js, review_dom_patch.js, and harness_presentation.js ← Review Checkpoint grouping/status, keyed DOM reconciliation, neutral harness identity presentation; read-side only
  │
  ├── supervisor/ ← Background thread inside server.py
  │   ├── active_activity.py ← Thread-safe registry (DirectActivityRegistry) for in-flight direct/ephemeral turns; feeds /api/state + WS typing frames
  │   ├── message_bus.py ← Queue-based local message bus (Web UI + reviewed transport skills)
  │   ├── workers.py ← Multiprocessing worker pool (fork/spawn by platform)
  │   ├── state.py ← Persistent state (state.json) with file locking
  │   ├── queue.py ← Task queue (PENDING/RUNNING) + activity-based timeout; exact-attempt main-LLM in-flight state spares only idle
  │   ├── cognitive_operations.py ← Typed active LLM/review/VLM operation leases for idle-rail; no scheduler or timing ledger
  │   ├── task_admission.py ← Admission-only helpers; token-owned reservations fence duplicate ingress; queue.py is authority
  │   ├── task_lifecycle.py ← Queue-owned budget fence + cancellation custody: EXCLUSIVE fenced claim, capture → verified death → completion-wins → settled write with delivery owed BEFORE settle → task_done; `sweep_cancel_intents` watchdog; `scope=cascade` settled only by no-live postcondition; `allow_settled_target` gates on `task_has_live_ownership` / `task_status.task_has_live_queue_ownership`; fenced Project deletion
  │   ├── cancel_publication.py ← Cancellation settlement PUBLICATION (one surface): typed CANCEL_* outcomes, salvage adapter, owed-before-settle outbox registration
  │   ├── queue_transitions.py ← Queue-owned TRANSITIONS: acceptance-fence open/inspect/seal, budget resume, typed evolution stop (`stop_evolution_tasks`, `evolution_owner_stopped`), fenced Project deletion
  │   ├── terminal_delivery.py ← Durable terminal-answer seam: `delivery_id` dedupe + PENDING outbox (`state/terminal_deliveries.json`); overflow → `terminal_delivery_exhausted`; reads STRICT; cascade digest by ANCESTRY
  │   ├── task_reaper.py ← Off-loop worker reaper: kill/join/archive/respawn timed-out workers (`task_reaper_wedged`)
  │   ├── owner_stop.py ← Graceful owner-stop episode: `finalize_then_cancel`, monotonic; `finalize_now` control (`owner_requested_finalization`); grace from `control_drained_at`, bounded by `OWNER_STOP_OUTER_CAP_SEC`; expiry → custody
  │   ├── schedule_time.py ← Cron/timezone schedule time parsing helpers
  │   ├── evolution_lifecycle.py ← Evolution campaign state + transaction lifecycle; cycle outcomes, cleanup, owner reports
  │   ├── events.py ← Event dispatcher (worker→supervisor) + exact attempt/round/call correlation; composes frozen subagent text (`_compose_subagent_text`); `EVENT_HANDLERS`, `emit_review_event`
  │   ├── chat_delivery_events.py ← Photo/video/document/link delivery handlers merged into `events.py` (`**_CDE`)
  │   ├── task_dispatch.py ← Pure construction of admitted supervisor task payload incl. depth + configured-route projections
  │   ├── log_addressing.py ← Explicit audience for task-scoped live log events (`address_task_event`, `make_server_log_sink`, `address_handler_push`; A2A suppressed at `push_log`)
  │   ├── steering.py ← Owner steering-message delivery to running tasks; typed refusal while cancel pending
  │   ├── telemetry_events.py ← Durable handlers for RARE typed telemetry events (`TELEMETRY_EVENT_HANDLERS`): `events.jsonl` passthrough + `task_message_injected`
  │   ├── git_ops.py ← Git operations (clone, checkout, rescue, rollback, push, credential helper) + bounded local-Git runner
  │   ├── update_source.py ← Official update source selection + network policy via bounded Git runner
  │   ├── update_recovery.py ← Exact owner Restore/promotion: pinned prior HEAD, rescue-before-reset
  │   ├── update_merge.py ← Managed-update engine: exact-target 3-way plan, clean fast-forward, reviewed assisted merge, transaction (`m0_tree`, `tests_evidence`, `stash_sha`), verified rollback/smoke
  │   ├── update_candidate.py ← Candidate/carrier primitives: worktree serialization, rerere-neutral flags, `failed-update-<target12>`, stash replay, `tests_evidence` proof
  │   └── update_merge_policy.py ← Presentation-only conflict labels; every conflict uses the same reviewed assisted path
  │
  └── ouroboros/ ← Agent core (runs inside worker processes)
      ├── config.py ← SSOT: paths, settings defaults, load/save, PID lock
      ├── secret_masking.py ← Exact Settings/MCP wire-placeholder emitters/recognizers + secret repair before env/persist
      ├── update_channels.py ← Closed Stable/QA/Development mapping + update-network defaults
      ├── colab_bootstrap.py ← Colab source-mode bootstrap: selected update source, `ouroboros` branch, Drive-backed settings/data
      ├── cli.py ← Source/headless CLI over gateway tasks, logs, settings, skills, marketplace, local-model, MCP
      ├── packaged_cli.py ← Packaged desktop CLI bridge: bundle roots, launcher-managed repo bootstrap, delegates to cli.py
      ├── packaged_cli_install.py ← Packaged CLI installer planning/execution for user-local shims
      ├── agent.py ← Task orchestrator (dispatch notes moved to `subagent_dispatch_notes.py`)
      ├── agent_startup_checks.py ← Startup verification and health checks
      ├── agent_task_pipeline.py ← Task pipeline orchestration; `swarm_efficiency` rollup (`lanes_requested`, never the used count); Swarm-intent `no_fanout_observed`; freezes subtree-cost snapshot before terminal
      ├── task_finalization.py ← Terminal delivery + sealed final ground truth; live final-answer before blocking post-task; one `delivery_id`
      ├── mutation_attribution.py ← Root-task baseline capture + clean-at-baseline Git candidate projection
      ├── process_interpreters.py ← Interpreter/runtime resolvers for four launch surfaces: unversioned-Python pre-guard; Node ladder (PATH-first + bundled fallback)
      ├── post_task_checkpoint.py ← Durable root post-task phase/final-cost checkpoint (finalization + Project naming)
      ├── presence_profile.py ← Strict reviewed `presence:` behavior profile parser
      ├── presence_runtime.py ← Symbolic `main`/`light` defaults + owner-local overrides
      ├── presence_capabilities.py ← Host-owned installation selections for portable Presence requests
      ├── presence_authority.py ← Immutable positive capability ceiling from one reviewed profile
      ├── presence_bindings.py ← Owner-created revocable transport-room → behavior-skill bindings
      ├── presence_admission.py ← Fresh review/enablement/profile admission + immutable per-turn snapshot
      ├── presence_context.py ← Presence instructions, exact event facts, declared topic projection
      ├── presence_runner.py ← Fresh-agent Presence turns; per-conversation serialization, idempotency
      ├── dialogue_provenance.py ← Shared exact transport provenance rendering (history, memory, consolidation)
      ├── extension_companion.py ← Host-supervised companion processes for transport skills
      ├── extension_reconcile_queue.py ← Durable worker→server reconcile markers + pickup loop
      ├── event_bus.py ← Typed in-process event bus for skill subscriptions
      ├── evolution_checkpoints.py ← Append-only campaign/eval checkpoint ledger
      ├── improvement_backlog.py ← Durable advisory backlog: dedup, ranking, close-on-commit, LLM grooming; parser-safe locked writer
      ├── loop.py ← LLM tool loop; no-op nudge; red/masked-verification nudges; `FINAL ANSWER:` latching; NANNY nudge (`NANNY_DELEGATED_RUN_FAILED`, `NANNY_METERED_OVERRUN`, charter facts `CONFIGURED_ACTOR_INCOMPLETE`/`CONFIGURED_ACTOR_UNKNOWN` via `subagent_bootstrap.actor_first_unresolved_fact`)
      ├── acceptance_dialogue.py ← Acceptance obligations/dialogue/decision machinery (from `loop.py`): `ACCEPTANCE_DECISION_REASONS`, `_set_acceptance_decision`, paid identity (`acceptance_paid_identity`)
      ├── loop_llm_call.py ← Single-round LLM call + usage accounting
      ├── delivery_protocol.py ← Delivery-finalization protocol vocabulary + pure parsers
      ├── task_pacing.py ← Task-pacing SSOT: deadlines, finalization reserve, BudgetSnapshot, acceptance rails
      ├── vision_routing.py ← Send-time image routing SSOT (`OUROBOROS_IMAGE_INPUT_MODE`, `OUROBOROS_MODEL_VISION`)
      ├── fallback_cooldown.py ← Per-process 429-aware cooldown for `OUROBOROS_MODEL_FALLBACKS`
      ├── model_concurrency.py ← Per-(model,use_local) semaphore capping CONCURRENT provider calls
      ├── project_naming.py ← SSOT for LLM-first project naming: LIGHT title + heuristic fallback
      ├── loop_tool_execution.py ← Tool dispatch + tool-result handling
      ├── deadline_utils.py ← Deadline parsing/remaining-time helpers + transport-vs-logical wait seam
      ├── observability.py ← Private forensic execution ledger: redaction, gzip CAS blobs, call manifests
      ├── cancel_intents.py ← Durable cancel-intent projection: locked `state/cancel_intents.json`, claim owner/pid + GENERATION fence, `scope` single-vs-cascade; ONE ingress `request_cancel`; reads STRICT (`CancelIntentProjectionCorrupt`); `allow_settled_target` exception
      ├── owner_hurry.py ← Owner "hurry" typed task-local latch; durable `owner_hurry` projection keyed by `task["_attempt"]`
      ├── owner_quiz.py ← Owner-quiz lifecycle projection: request-id-idempotent `record_answered`, structural `reconcile_terminal`
      ├── routing_wait.py ← SSOT of durable routing-receipt waits (`wait_for_promotion_admission`, `wait_for_routing_annotation`)
      ├── outcomes.py ← Typed task-outcome + acceptance-decision authority (axes kept separate)
      ├── outcome_receipt_store.py ← Durable verification-receipt path/append/read authority + exact-row union
      ├── depth_evidence.py ← Host-visible depth projection from persisted child provenance
      ├── _outcome_receipts.py ← Private receipt identity helpers: `receipt_canonical_identity` → `ReceiptIdentity`, `reconciliation_identity`, `IDENTITY_KINDS` + `KIND_NORMALIZES_COMMAND_TEXT` (closed per-kind identity table), outstanding sets, disclosed projections (bounds never silent)
      ├── _outcome_tool_errors.py ← Leaf SSOT for tool-trace status vocabularies + execution-axis classification
      ├── code_intelligence.py ← Code inventory v2: derived facts, polyglot extraction (tree-sitter / stdlib ast), fallback, incremental cache
      ├── code_search_rg.py ← Optional ripgrep-backed search_code helper; matches post-filtered through gates
      ├── pricing.py ← Exact-route best-effort provider-catalog lookup with nullable estimates
      ├── usage_accounting.py ← Append-only physical-model-attempt monetary authority: reserve/dispatch/settle/release, cross-process lock, quarantine
      ├── _usage_rows.py ← Pure row arithmetic for summaries, breakdowns, Skill Review projection
      ├── _usage_rows_memo.py ← In-process read acceleration: validated-rows memo + render cache
      ├── skill_review_usage.py ← Read-only cached projection of final physical-attempt rows per wave
      ├── usage_ledger.py ← Durable append-only ledger SUBSTRATE: locking, atomic append+fsync, validation, quarantine
      ├── cost_projection.py ← ONE SSOT cost projection: `accounted_upper_bound_usd` (`cost_usd` deprecated alias), null never $0.00, `COST_OPENNESS_FIELDS` markers
      ├── delegate_custody.py ← Durable custody for delegated runs: `delegate_run_*` rows + `containment_faults.jsonl`; OWNED/FOREIGN/UNKNOWN; `Idempotency-Key` per intention; `retry_of` replay; typed cancel vocabulary; `daemon_says_absent`; apply-intent protocol
      ├── delegate_source_coverage.py ← Oversized-work-order source custody: interval union, strict receipts, cannot-verify terminal
      ├── delegate_evidence.py ← Read-side execution-evidence projection (`task_execution_evidence`) + stamp/attempt writers (`record_nanny_nudge_stamp` reads/writes the task-scoped `NANNY_NUDGE_STAMP`)
      ├── synthesis_cost_text.py ← Synthesis-prompt cost/outcome snapshot renderers
      ├── llm.py ← Multi-provider LLM routing; canonical conversations function-shaped, send delegates to request-wire leaves
      ├── openai_chat_custom.py ← Direct-OpenAI Chat function→custom codec; canonical response normalization; no Responses transcript
      ├── openai_chat_dispatch.py ← Direct-OpenAI Chat policy: custom first, function fallback, explicit `none` only under attempt rail
      ├── request_wire_contract.py ← Provider-neutral exact-route profile; closed `set_value`/`drop_field`/`replace_dialect` actions
      ├── request_wire_resolution.py ← Deterministic composition of exact-profile actions; contradiction fail-open
      ├── request_wire_receipts.py ← Factory-bound candidates + semantic-success receipts
      ├── request_wire_attempt.py ← Physical-attempt validation + public `usage.request_wire` disclosure
      ├── request_wire_custom_validation.py ← Parser-issued exact-schema receipts; never authorizes execution
      ├── request_wire_recovery.py ← Same-route exception/body recovery state machine under attempt authority
      ├── anthropic_native_custody.py ← Private direct-Anthropic assistant-content receipt for unfinished tool turns
      ├── reasoning_artifacts.py ← Shape-first classification of replayed OpenRouter reasoning (sealed vs portable)
      ├── llm_observability.py ← Observed non-loop calls through public `LLMClient`
      ├── llm_probe.py ← Bounded one-shot LLM probes (capability evidence + Provider Test)
      ├── mcp_client.py ← HTTP/SSE/stdio MCP client manager; normalizes `mcp_<server>__<tool>`
      ├── safety.py ← Policy-based LLM safety check; `_SAFETY_CONTEXT_CHAR_BUDGET`; rate-limit blocks with `⚠️ SAFETY_UNAVAILABLE`
      ├── consciousness.py ← Background thinking loop (with progress emission)
      ├── consolidator.py ← Block-wise dialogue consolidation (dialogue_blocks.json)
      ├── memory.py ← Scratchpad, identity, chat history
      ├── project_facts.py ← Per-project facts store (`projects/<id>/knowledge`), isolated from global memory
      ├── task_tree_ledger.py ← Task-tree coordination ledger keyed by `root_task_id` (`tree_note`/`tree_read`, `headless.prune_task_trees`)
      ├── projects_registry.py ← Multi-project registry: durable `data/state/projects.json`, immutable identity, lifecycle/tombstone, routing fence
      ├── project_dialogue.py ← Read-only Project dialogue lens + annotations; `needs_manual_target` picker authority
      ├── project_lease.py ← One-writer-per-project lease (`assign_tasks`)
      ├── context.py ← LLM context-source builder; `_capture_context_core` places `## Available subagents`
      ├── context_residency.py ← Class-aware residency classifiers for self-body docs (ARCHITECTURE/DEVELOPMENT full vs nav map); leaf of context.py
      ├── main_context_authority.py ← Main-only defensive continuation projection
      ├── client_surface.py ← Owner Surface Fact SSOT: bounded normalizer for sending-surface observables
      ├── context_fit.py ← Main task-local fit authority: Max/Low projections from one immutable core
      ├── context_budget.py ← Context-window budget vocabulary + typed reclaim SSOT
      ├── capability_evidence.py ← Sourced, route-fingerprinted context-window EVIDENCE
      ├── context_layout.py ← Reference-doc form SSOT (Max: ARCHITECTURE class-scoped — self-body full, direct-chat/external nav map + pointer; Low: nav map for every class)
      ├── context_compaction.py ← Requested complete-input reclaim materializer; transactional apply
      ├── context_health.py ← Leaf owner of health-invariant assembly + probes; re-exports `build_health_invariants`
      ├── headless.py ← Headless task child-drive isolation, workspace patch artifacts, memory export
      ├── workspace_patch_rules.py ← PURE patch/snapshot eligibility rules (credential veto, junk exclusion)
      ├── coop_checkpoint.py ← Quiescent checkpoint commits for dirty host-minted genesis/coop roots
      ├── delegate_output.py ← Staged-output + read-receipt for delegated runs (`delegated_runs/<run>.json` whole detail)
      ├── delegate_containment.py ← Containment verification for one delegated run (access + HOME facts)
      ├── delegate_progress.py ← Low-level bounded Claudexor poll/progress observation
      ├── nanny_pacing.py ← Configured-session pacing marks (`BASELINE_RESET_TOOLS`, delegate verbs)
      ├── delegate_interactions.py ← Interactive-question cluster: `waiting_on_user` payload, `_delegate_answer` typed outcomes
      ├── delegate_shared.py ← Shared nanny-verb leaf: typed refusal, custody-rooted emit, `_owned_run`
      ├── route_spec.py ← Neutral route primitive shared by Available subagents + reviewer rows
      ├── configured_subagents.py ← Canonical `OUROBOROS_SUBAGENTS` parser/serializer, stable ids, fingerprinting
      ├── subagent_runtime.py ← Configured-actor runtime: immutable snapshots, exact `subagent_id`, API/session dispatch
      ├── subagent_work_order.py ← Complete external work-order compiler; 250,000-char wire budget, never a prefix
      ├── subagent_bootstrap.py ← Session-nanny pre-round bootstrap: START leaf BEFORE first metered round (`configured_session_started`)
      ├── delegate_supervision.py ← Event-only sleeping-nanny loop; quiet windows renew without a model call
      ├── delegate_start_instructions.py ← Stable harness host instructions + bounded coordination appendix
      ├── delegate_recovery.py ← Exact-leaf recovery for proven crash / planned self-restart; vetoes no-resume causes
      ├── delegate_registration_policy.py ← Registration persistence: `persistent_registration` survives retire paths
      ├── delegate_pending.py ← Durable pending-invocation replay (idempotency key + canonical body)
      ├── delegate_terminal.py ← Task-scoped delegated-run reconciliation + custody audit persistence
      ├── subagent_dispatch_notes.py ← Child-facing compatibility dispatch notes + typed blocked outcome
      ├── subagent_messages.py ← Bounded durable child-message identity (final frames, recovery, replay)
      ├── subagents.py ← Structured lineage/usage envelopes + bounded legacy compatibility
      ├── subagent_worktrees.py ← Acting self_worktree lifecycle: provision/remove/prune isolated worktrees + registry; genesis (`provision_genesis_project`); C1 snapshots (`provision_execution_snapshot`, `provision_payload_snapshot`, `refs/ouroboros/delegated/`); GC only by custody proof
      ├── artifacts.py ← Task-scoped artifact helpers for tools, outputs, chat media, finalization (`artifact_store/attachments/`, `chat_media/`, `.scratch_manifest.json`, `delegated_runs/`)
      ├── retention.py ← Unified GC retention SSOT (clamp/age-cutoff + legacy seed picker)
      ├── workspace_preflight.py ← Read-only external-workspace git/manifest/toolchain snapshot
      ├── project_sources.py ← Project working-folder sources: attach existing folder (validated, NEVER auto-init) or server-side clone
      ├── promotion_source.py ← Supervisor-side promoted-task source admission
      ├── workspace_admission.py ← Shared workspace validation/admission (`workspace_admission.validate_workspace_root()`, `workspace="none"`)
      ├── local_model.py ← Local LLM lifecycle (llama-cpp-python)
      ├── local_model_autostart.py ← Local model startup helper
      ├── deep_self_review.py ← Deep self-review: Atlas context + full memory whitelist → 1M-context model
      ├── review.py ← Code collection, complexity metrics, pre-commit review
      ├── review_execution_projection.py ← Read-side presentation leaf: `executions[]` + bounded finding rows
      ├── preflight_runner.py ← Hermetic reviewed-change pytest gate: disposable worktree, hardened candidate capture, env scrub; `LANE_EXCLUSION_EXPR` / `PARALLEL_PASS_FLAGS` SSOT
      ├── preflight_node.py ← Node lane of hermetic commit gate (`node --test tests/*.test.js`)
      ├── review_substrate.py ← Reviewer-slot coordinator: duplicate ids independent slots (`ReviewAssignment`, `api_chat`/`agent_session`, `ReviewRouteUnavailable`, `ReviewRouteKind`, `ReviewAttemptResult`, `GET /v2/harnesses`)
      ├── review_custody.py ← Process-local physical review-worker custody
      ├── review_owner_custody.py ← Causal bridge from process-custody identity to commit-attempt ledger
      ├── review_execution.py ← Review execution below the seam: closed route vocabulary, immutable `ReviewAssignment`, D19 verdict order
      ├── review_native_episode.py ← Bounded native tool-round review executor (`NativeToolRoundReviewExecutor`; read-only registry, caps fail closed)
      ├── review_verdict_extraction.py ← Session/native verdict canonicalization
      ├── review_session_custody.py ← Exact delegated-review recovery validation + pre-POST checkpoint
      ├── review_slot_cancel.py ← Cancel-honesty helpers for hosted review slots (verified outcomes only)
      ├── commit_admission.py ← Deterministic commit-admission preflights SSOT (P9 + syntax + preflight proof)
      ├── reviewer_slot_config.py ← Structured reviewer-slot SSOT: stable slot ids, route targets, effort
      ├── review_state.py ← Durable advisory pre-review state (advisory_review.json)
      ├── review_cycles.py ← Shared paid-review-cycle cap SSOT (`OUROBOROS_REVIEW_MAX_CYCLES`, `unlimited`)
      ├── review_dispatch.py ← Reviewer dispatch primitives: slot-id mint + write-ahead PAID stamp (`ReviewPaidStamp`)
      ├── reviewer_window.py ← Reviewer context-window SSOT per ROUTE (`ReviewerWindow`, `blocking_authority_allowed`)
      ├── triad_review.py ← Shared multi-model review primitives: JSON extraction, quorum accounting
      ├── onboarding_wizard.py ← Shared desktop/web onboarding bootstrap + validation
      ├── subscription_install_presets.py ← PURE sibling install compilers (task-actor + reviewer presets)
      ├── settings_setup_contract.py ← SSOT for Settings/Onboarding setup contract
      ├── owner_mailbox.py ← Per-task user message mailbox (compat module name)
      ├── launcher_bootstrap.py ← Bundle-to-repo bootstrap + managed sync helpers
      ├── launcher_server_reaper.py ← POSIX same-install server discovery/reap for PID-lock owner
      ├── provider_models.py ← Provider-specific model IDs + direct-provider defaults + active/legacy key split
      ├── runtime_mode_policy.py ← Runtime-mode protected-path policy (safety files, frozen contracts, invariants)
      ├── schedule_contract.py ← Schedule id, 5-field cron, IANA timezone validation SSOT
      ├── reflection.py ← Execution reflection and pattern capture
      ├── post_task_evolution.py ← Post-task self-evolution: worker promotion signal, supervisor applies via gated enqueuer
      ├── repo_remotes.py ← GitHub remote provisioning: `managed` read-only, `origin` optional persistence
      ├── review_evidence.py ← Bounded provenance-tagged task-acceptance evidence builder (`integrate_delegated_patch`)
      ├── review_evidence_refs.py ← Leaf SSOT for acceptance evidence-reference vocabulary + exact resolver
      ├── semantic_dedup.py ← LLM-first semantic-duplicate detector for free-text items (fail-open)
      ├── betterleaks_runtime.py ← Betterleaks 1.8.1 pin/resolver installer SSOT; Publish never downloads
      ├── skill_loader.py ← Skill discovery + durable skill state (walks data/skills/*, persists health)
      ├── skill_readiness.py ← Central readiness helper: review + hash + enablement + grants → verdict
      ├── skill_dependencies.py ← Shared dependency-spec resolution for skill payloads
      ├── skill_repair_admission.py ← Hash-bound skill-repair admission: CAS against own payload state
      ├── skill_publish_snapshot.py ← Immutable captured-byte authority for publication binding
      ├── skill_publish_scanner.py ← Redaction-safe Betterleaks adapter over named bytes
      ├── skill_publish_result.py ← Bounded typed attempt/receipt transport + finalization veto
      ├── skill_publish_github.py ← GitHub transport after local gates (non-overwriting branch/PR)
      ├── skill_publish_eligibility.py ← Passive visibility + ordinary-task admission SSOT
      ├── skill_review_status.py ← Skill-review verdict aggregation SSOT (enforcement maps to executable_review)
      ├── skill_review_passes.py ← Skill-review pass runner (multi-model or chunked per-pack)
      ├── skill_review.py ← Skill review pipeline: preflight + optional advisory + tri-model trust gate
      ├── skill_review_history.py ← Append-only Skill Review history with job-idempotent terminal rows
      ├── skill_review_cycles.py ← Max-Review-Cycles semantics for skill review (paid-cycle counting, $0 replay)
      ├── extension_loader.py ← Phase 4 loader for type: extension skills (in-process / child-proxy, atomic unload)
      ├── extension_process_runner.py ← Child-process runner for isolated-dep/native extensions (scrubbed env, caps)
      ├── extension_ui_validation.py ← Host-owned recursive declarative-schema-v1 validator
      ├── extension_isolated_deps.py ← Per-extension isolated-dep bridge (production deps only in child)
      ├── extension_health.py ← Durable per-extension health vector (`health.json`)
      ├── skill_token.py ← Opaque Host Service API token wrapper for reviewed skills
      ├── marketplace/ ← ClawHub + OuroborosHub marketplace package (`clawhub.py`, `ouroboroshub.py`, adopt transaction, `fetcher.py`, `adapter.py`, `install.py`, `provenance.py`)
      ├── skill_lifecycle_queue.py ← Single FIFO lane for mutating skill lifecycle actions + snapshot
      ├── skill_review_runner.py ← Shared lifecycle-backed skill review runner (API + tool paths)
      ├── server_auth.py ← Non-localhost auth gate (`OUROBOROS_NETWORK_PASSWORD`)
      ├── server_control.py ← Process-control helpers: restart, panic stop
      ├── server_entrypoint.py ← CLI argument parsing, port-binding helpers
      ├── server_runtime.py ← Server startup/onboarding + WebSocket liveness helpers
      ├── server_web.py ← Static web file helpers (NoCacheStaticFiles, web dir resolver)

      ├── process_containment.py ← `ProcessContainer` for hermetic gate: env-token membership read from live kernel state
      ├── platform_layer.py ← Cross-platform process/path/locking helpers + Windows Job Object ABI

      ouroboros/process_custody.py ← Supervised spawning + durable orphan ledger
      (v6.26.0): `spawn_supervised()` records long-lived children in
      `data/state/process_ledger.jsonl` ({pid, pgid, fingerprint{start_time,
      cmd_sha256}, purpose, scope, owner_task, session_id}); the reaper (startup
      + 10-min tick) prunes entries whose owner died, by STRICT fingerprint
      only — never command-line class. Fingerprint start time is a DOWNGRADE-
      SAFE pair: unversioned `start_time` (legacy `ps -o lstart=` spelling) +
      versioned `start_time_boot` (preferred; Linux `/proc` boot-qualified
      `"<ticks>.<boot_hex>"`); mint order boot-qualified first, then wall-clock,
      then `"<ticks>."` as disclosed last resort. Sibling-free mismatches verify
      one `ps` legacy comparison; boot-sibling rows judged by boot evidence
      alone; bare ticks never authorize a kill against boot evidence. Current
      session processes take a cheap liveness check (recycled kept one gen,
      pruned next, never killed); stale/foreign use full fingerprints; DEAD
      pids fall to service-group evidence. `daemon` entries kept; skill
      companions (`purpose companion:<skill>:<name>`) log-only by default
      (`enforce_companion_reap=False` → `process_would_reap`), fail-safe
      (unknown live set ⇒ keep-all). `start_parent_lifeline()` gives our
      entrypoints a ppid watchdog that group-suicides when the parent dies.
      Panic layers (`_active_subprocesses`, port sweeps, Windows Job Objects)
      are unchanged complements.

# Build, CI & public metadata (not part of runtime)
.github/workflows/ci.yml     ← Five-tier CI (quick / full / integration / skill smoke / build+release)
.github/workflows/claudexor-platform-gate.yml ← 3-OS delegated-execution gate: fixture lane (offline, $0) + live lane on explicit keys
.github/workflows/dependency-graph.yml ← Pin-derived dependency snapshot for bundled Claudexor runtime
.github/workflows/scorecard.yml ← OpenSSF Scorecard on `main` pushes + weekly (full SHA pins, read-only defaults)
CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md            ← Contributor Covenant 3.0 community rules + moderator contact
CITATION.cff                  ← Machine-readable citation + preferred six-author technical-report citation
docs/benchmarks/evidence.json ← Release-bound non-GAIA projection of public benchmark claims + evidence links
site/paper/index.html         ← Canonical paper landing page (ScholarlyArticle JSON-LD, evidence navigation)
scripts/claudexor_platform_smoke.py ← platform gate smoke runner (daemon boot, one delegated task, containment report)
scripts/validate_scope_receipt.py   ← contributor-facing scope-checklist receipt validator (over `scope_review_contract.normalize_scope_items`)
scripts/fetch_claudexor_runtime.py  ← release-build fetcher for pinned managed Claudexor runtime (claudexor_runtime_pin.json)
build.sh                      ← macOS build (PyInstaller → .dmg)
build_linux.sh                ← Linux build (portable-Python PyInstaller → .AppImage + .tar.gz)
scripts/build_appimage.sh     ← AppDir assembly + digest-pinned appimagetool + embedded runtime
packaging/appimage/           ← AppRun desktop/CLI dispatch + Linux desktop metadata
packaging/systemd/            ← Opt-in native-package user unit; unit starts launcher, never owns restart policy
scripts/build_linux_packages.sh ← Wraps x86_64 payload into `.deb`, `.rpm`, RED OS `.red80.rpm` assets
scripts/smoke_linux_packages.sh ← Release-gating apt/dnf CLI + unit + desktop-launcher smoke (Ubuntu/Fedora; vendor lane informational)
build_windows.ps1             ← Windows build (PyInstaller → .zip)
scripts/build_repo_bundle.py  ← Builds `repo.bundle` + `repo_bundle_manifest.json` for packaged releases
scripts/run_external_review.py ← dual-lane non-committing review wrapper; `--contributor` reviews exact proposal with blocking enforcement, emits redacted SHA-bound packet; `READY_FOR_INTEGRATION` is evidence, never merge authority
scripts/contributor_review_evidence.py ← route-neutral contributor-packet binding: content-addressed projections, stable slot ids, provenance without claiming observed facts, fails closed on typed contradictions
scripts/run_plan_review.py ← operator plan-review tool running the SAME engine as `plan_task`; prints recorded wave + coordinated output, untruncated; review-exempt dev tool
scripts/cleanup_test_pollution.py ← Dry-run-first cleanup utility for local test-pollution artifacts
skills/telegram/            ← Bundled owner-only Telegram bridge + optional Mini App gateway; seeded disabled until grants approved
skills/unix_computer_use/   ← Bundled extension skill for supervised desktop observation/input; disabled connection fails closed
packaging/cli/                ← Packaged CLI shell/cmd wrappers + user-local installer launchers
Dockerfile                    ← Docker image (web UI runtime)
site/                         ← Public GitHub Pages source (Vite); `sync-assets.mjs` copies assets; `pnpm build` renders `docs/`
devtools/                     ← Tracked operator tooling outside runtime/package discovery; no runtime import; sentinel-marked isolated roots may suppress rotation/hot-store warnings
devtools/benchmarks/cybergym/ ← Pinned Level-1 CyberGym launcher, adapter, settings, and methodology; detailed topology stays in the benchmark docs
```
### Devtools boundary

`devtools/` is tracked operator tooling outside runtime and package discovery. Runtime, web, and release code must not import it; a touched devtool still receives normal repository review. Generated operator artifacts belong in an explicit external output root, never `repo/` or live `data/`. Domain-specific architecture, launch instructions, methodology stay with the devtool, not in this core map. A sentinel-marked isolated devtool root may suppress progress-log rotation and hot-store growth warnings because its external reader owns a bounded run-local lifecycle; code owns the sentinel; the exception must remain narrow.

### Gateway Boundary v1

`ouroboros/gateway/` is the single inbound HTTP/WebSocket boundary for browser and CLI clients. `gateway/contracts.py` owns the active envelopes and endpoint index; `gateway/router.py` owns route collection; `gateway/files.py` and `gateway/host_service.py` keep separately mounted trust boundaries; `contracts/api_v1.py` remains a compatibility re-export only. Domain handlers translate transport into calls on existing runtime owners and must not acquire a second copy of queue, review, settings, or lifecycle policy. `gateway/owner_settings.py` is the one owner-scoped settings WRITE seam (generic settings POST, single-decision owner endpoints — runtime mode, auto-grant, context mode, safety mode — and the onboarding transaction); membership is calling `_owner_write_settings`. The settings lock is a write precondition: a timed-out acquisition refuses before any precondition or write; an optional precondition is proved under the lock; a `CommitBoundary` marks the instant the bytes land, so a failure in a LATER step is reported as that step failing, never as "nothing was saved". `saved` is a field on both sides: every pre-commit refusal answers via `unsaved_error` with `saved:false`; once a post-commit failure says `saved:true`, an envelope that merely omits the field cannot be told apart from an old or truncated one.

Frontend requests go through `web/modules/api_client.js`; the JSDoc mirror `web/modules/api_types.js` plus gateway parity tests keep the build-free client contract visible. Extension HTTP routes stay under `/api/extensions/<skill>/...`; extension WebSocket events use the namespaced dispatch contract. Facade reason: the UI may change without importing the agent body; runtime internals may change without ad-hoc browser contracts.

### CLI / Headless Boundary

`ouroboros.cli` is a first-class client of the same gateway, queue, scheduler, supervisor, and durable task-result owners as the web UI — not a second task engine; the parser is the command-surface SSOT. Task-streaming commands reserve stdout for the final answer, patch, result object, or JSONL stream and send human progress to stderr.

Headless transport: `POST /api/tasks` admits an ordinary managed root; `GET /api/tasks` returns a non-materializing list projection; `GET /api/tasks/<id>` reads the effective durable result; `GET /api/tasks/<id>/events` performs the archive-aware replay/live SSE flow; `GET /api/tasks/<id>/artifacts/<name>` serves a declared artifact or exact content-addressed outbound chat media. Artifact names are simple filenames confined to `data/task_results/artifacts/<task_id>/` (a stored arbitrary path is not a download capability). External task admission cannot mint child authority: the CLI refuses any `delegation_role` other than `root`; the gateway rejects caller-supplied lineage, subagent labels, and internal task types; only the internal `schedule_subagent` path creates children. Admission reserves the task id and worker slot first, then persists queue snapshot and scheduled result under one lock; failure rolls back only the token-owned row with a loud typed refusal. Attachments are copied into the effective task drive before enqueue.

Workspace tasks default to `memory_mode=forked`; `shared` is rejected for an external workspace. A project-scoped task without an external workspace may retain `memory_mode=shared` as the recorded request, but the gateway materializes it on a forked child drive so project data isolation does not depend on relabelling. `--detach` returns only after durable admission. Stream and poll waiters treat a lifecycle result as terminal only after artifact state has left `pending`/`finalizing`. `ouroboros run` exits successfully only for lifecycle `completed`, execution `ok`, no failed/degraded objective, and no failed/missing/pending/finalizing artifact bundle; `--patch`/`--patch-out` are stricter — a failed or missing patch, a no-change result, an empty payload, or unfinished artifact finalization is an error, so shell automation never reads "the model answered" as "the deliverable exists".

CLI schedules and skill-manifest schedules enqueue ordinary supervisor tasks (no parallel scheduler); a blank timezone means the DST-aware system zone; the active schedule digest is task/consciousness context.

Packaged artifacts ship a tiny wrapper and installer rather than a second PyInstaller runtime. `ouroboros.packaged_cli` locates `repo.bundle`, bootstraps the launcher-managed checkout, then invokes the same `ouroboros.cli` with the embedded interpreter and canonical app/repo/data environment. Packaged `server` is refused (it would bypass launcher-owned bootstrap). Packaged `run --start` is loopback-only: it starts the outer desktop application when no ready gateway exists and waits for both `/api/health` and `supervisor_ready`. A normal Linux FUSE relaunch retains direct `AppRun → launcher` execution; a nested extract-and-run relaunch receives a private temporary base whose marker-gated `AppRun` acts as custodian (waits for the launcher, removes the verified extraction and the empty private base, then returns the payload status).

Release builds carry Node and ripgrep. Skill-side Node consumers go through `node_runtime.select_skill_node_runtime()` (bundled-first, health-rollback to PATH); the four generic process launch surfaces run the opposite policy (`process_interpreters.resolve_process_node`): a healthy PATH candidate stays byte-identical; the bundled runtime substitutes only when the PATH candidate is missing or probe-dead, rewriting only bare `node`/`nodejs` argv and attesting a child-env PATH prepend. Neither preference is mode-gated. Every bundled consumer searches the one `platform_layer.bundled_resource_bases()` chain (explicit `OUROBOROS_BUNDLE_DIR`, frozen process root, interpreter-ancestor roots, source checkout) — the explicit handoff is necessary because server/CLI children run from the managed repo without the launcher's `_MEIPASS`.

The embedded interpreter must never write into the signed application: launcher and packaged-CLI entry processes suppress bytecode before project imports; `launcher_bootstrap.embedded_python_env()` redirects child bytecode and user installs to `data/state/` and clears inherited `PYTHONNOUSERSITE`; `platform_layer.pip_install_target_args()` adds `--user` only for `python-standalone`. Disclosed residual: `data/state/python-userbase` sits ahead of the bundle's site-packages and is never pruned or versioned — an older release's dependency can shadow a newer signed copy until the owner removes the directory manually.

A workspace binding changes the contextual repository used by code/file tools; it never replaces the system repository as the source of BIBLE, architecture, development policy, prompts, native skills, or review governance. `/api/tasks` and project-room promotion share `workspace_admission.validate_workspace_root()`: the path must exist, be the exact root of a git worktree, and remain disjoint from the system repo and data drive under resolved, bidirectional, case-folded overlap checks. A set-but-broken project folder fails loudly rather than degrading into a workspace-less self-modification task. Promotion treats an empty project binding idempotently as a provisioned standalone git repository; a non-empty invalid path still fails loudly.

Direct gateway workspace admission performs a read-only preflight over the real target: git state and bounded porcelain rows, manifests and script commands, curated plus manifest-inferred tool availability (labelled `tools_on_path`/`tools_missing_from_path`; `shutil.which` measures PATH PRESENCE, not executability), and PATH bootstrap dirs. The full report is `workspace_preflight.json`; prompts and metadata get only the bounded summary. Project-room promotion runs the same probe under an eight-second cap on the event-drain path; timeout degrades to an explicit skipped snapshot.

Workspace completion compares against the captured preflight base, so ordinary task-local commits remain part of the deliverable delta. An acting child's patch is bound to `task_constraint.base_sha`; in a shared tree the parent's own commits move HEAD, so `base_sha` stays the patch base and integration relies on reverse-patch shared-workspace verification. An unborn repository compares against Git's canonical empty tree. Patch capture streams tracked binary diff plus admitted untracked files; scratch-matched, cache/build/junk, oversized or binary untracked blobs, and an incidental lockfile without its manifest change are excluded with reasons; a sensitive-looking untracked credential blocks the patch entirely. `workspace_patch.json` is written for EVERY workspace finalization (including no-change and failed), recording status, identities, patch size/hash/diffstat, included/excluded counts and reasons — the truth source for strict CLI patch behavior; `workspace.patch` exists only for `ready_with_changes`.

Forked and empty task state lives under `data/state/headless_tasks/<task_id>/data`. Forked mode copies `identity.md`, `WORLD.md`, `registry.md`; a non-project task also gets the global `memory/knowledge/` tree; a project-scoped fork omits global topic knowledge but keeps the cross-project Pattern Register. Dialogue blocks, scratchpad, owner mailbox, and task history never cross the boundary.

The child drive is execution state, not the durable handoff. On completion the child result is copied to the canonical task-result root and declared artifacts are rebased into the canonical artifact store (missing source = copy failure; name collisions get deterministic suffixes). Verification-receipt replicas are unioned with exact-row de-duplication before copy-back. When the canonical root's `post_task_synthesis` has a terminal value, both late copy-back and effective reads pass canonical and child records through the same pure field-custody projection: child-owned acceptance may enrich the result, while parent-owned terminal post-task markers and cost/round/token fields cannot be overwritten by a stale child replica. `memory_export.json` is an explicit artifact containing the child memory snapshot, never auto-merged.

Continuation authority uses an explicit `predecessor_task_id` (`""` = fresh; omission/null refused before lookup, enqueue, or spend). An authored root summary is persisted with its exact source at the result owner; Project child-born text is promoted before child-drive collection, while a non-Project parent-born text wins a later child replica overlay. Main builds a defensive provider-only copy: only oversized `result`/`final_answer` strings cross the inline threshold, resolved from persisted narrative, a bounded legacy authored row, or an explicit source-resolvable gap. The automatic startup injection is a bounded continuation ENVELOPE (not a body copy): every compact terminal fact inherits by copy; the predecessor's operative contract core inherits without its nested `predecessor_authority`; every field is whole-or-pointer against one strict tool-result budget; sha256/chars ride with their observation moment. Durable `task_results` bodies are the untouched SSOT.

System self-modification, external workspace delivery, and `genesis` remain distinct: reviewed system changes land through the commit gate; external workspace work leaves the selected target tree and returns its patch/manifest; an acting genesis child builds a durable standalone repository under the configured projects root and also receives `deliverable_manifest.json` (bounded content listing, streamed hashes to 64 MiB, size-only disclosure above). Project-local dependencies are ordinary target work; global/system installation remains runtime-policy reviewed; `sudo` is always non-interactive.

Startup garbage collection uses the common retention policy and removes a headless child drive only when the canonical parent result is terminal, artifact finalization is terminal, the retention window has elapsed, and the recorded child path matches. Canonical task results, artifacts, durable genesis/project repositories, and explicit memory exports survive — which is why all information required after child-drive deletion must cross the canonical handoff before a task is presented as settled.

### Runtime topology

Two continuity roles: the release-reviewed `launcher.py` owns the single-instance PID lock, packaged bundle bootstrap, server process, desktop/browser presentation, restart signal, and final cleanup; its running packaged copy is outside the managed repository it supervises, so replacement requires a new application artifact. `server.py` is the self-editable inner runtime (HTTP/WebSocket, gateway, message bus, supervisor, durable queue, worker pool); managed updates replace this inner body without destroying the outer process that can finish, retry, or roll back the transition.

Native `.deb`/`.rpm` add an opt-in systemd user unit — an alternate ingress to the same launcher, not a third runtime role; it deliberately has no restart policy (the launcher owns managed restart, the crash fuse, panic-to-complete-stop), and `KillMode=control-group` lets an explicit unit stop reach the whole tree.

On POSIX the launcher starts `server.py` in a new session/process group; on Windows it creates the process suspended, assigns a kill-on-close Job Object, then resumes (failure to establish Job custody refuses to run the child). It writes `data/state/server_process.json` (PID, pgid, paths, ports, argv, creation time) and, before group cleanup, re-proves that the PID is live, is not the launcher, belongs to the recorded group, has the expected server/repo identity and a matching command line; a stale or non-matching record is removed without killing the unrelated PID.

Holding the PID lock licenses the launcher to reap leftover generations of its own install. The sweep runs at `main()` preflight and the top of every lifecycle generation — recorded-process cleanup, then stray sweep, then port sweep. A PID is proven only on three live kernel facts: the exact `<REPO_DIR>/server.py` argv token after a python interpreter token, `OUROBOROS_DATA_DIR` equal to this data directory, and `OUROBOROS_MANAGED_BY_LAUNCHER=1`; each proof is revalidated immediately before the signal (descendants captured first), bounded to three kill passes. Kills additionally require a byte-exact environment source (/proc); hosts without /proc are report-only. An unreadable environment, a missing marker, or a different data directory is never a license to kill — those processes are spared and named in the launcher log. Only the fresh generation-loop result refuses to start a colliding server. The sweep is POSIX-only, never runs on panic or window-close paths, and deliberately does not consult the custody ledger (missing ledger entries are the defect being repaired).

Extension children, delegated runtimes, supervised task services, local-model processes, and skill companions add processes beneath these roles; every long-lived runtime-owned process must enter its custody ledger or group so shutdown, cancellation, panic, and restart can account for it after the originating worker disappears. The owner's external applications are outside this tree by design.

The lifecycle thread removes stale port state, starts the server, follows the actual port file, and waits for health. Exit code 42 requests a managed restart (bundle metadata refresh, dependency sync, new inner body). A dependency install failure pauses five seconds and retries once; a second failure is logged and startup proceeds under the existing crash fuse (silent pip loss would make a later ImportError inexplicable; refusing every offline restart would break a checkout whose dependencies are already present). Five ordinary crashes within 120 seconds stop automatic restart. Panic exit performs full cleanup and terminates the outer process rather than entering that retry loop.

On Linux the browser fallback checks `DISPLAY`/`WAYLAND_DISPLAY` before touching pywebview, then calls pywebview's `webview.guilib.initialize()` selector, preserving `PYWEBVIEW_GUI`; for GTK, `Gdk.Display.get_default()` must also return a live display. Disclosed Qt residual: after a display variable exists, Qt is trusted without constructing a second `QGuiApplication`, because probing it can cause the crash the check is meant to avoid. When the probe fails, the same launcher still owns and supervises the same `server.py`; only presentation changes — it prints the bound URL, best-effort opens the default browser on a daemon thread, and serves first-run setup through the existing blocking `/api/onboarding` web overlay. A repeated launch that loses the PID lock re-reads the port file, soft-polls health ~10s, then prints the last-read loopback URL and best-effort opens it with a bounded join. Missing Git or failed health on the lock-owning startup reports the cause/log paths and exits nonzero. Browser-mode SIGINT/SIGTERM handlers are installed before the lifecycle thread can spawn the server and only set the shutdown event; if the lifecycle thread dies through the crash fuse without a shutdown request, the main thread performs the same cleanup and exits nonzero. `sys.exit` paths leave PID-lock release to the registered `atexit` owner.

A pre-existing cross-platform residual remains: shutdown admission is not atomic with publishing the newly spawned child — a signal in the short interval between `Popen` and recording the child can leave that process for the later orphan sweep. This is tracked rather than hidden.

### Data layout (`~/Ouroboros/`)

```
~/Ouroboros/
├── repo/              ← agent's self-modifying git repository
│   ├── server.py      ← running server (launcher-managed git clone)
│   ├── ouroboros/      ← agent core package (see §1 tree)
│   ├── supervisor/     ← supervisor package (see §1 tree)
│   ├── web/            ← Web UI files (SPA + web/modules/*)
│   ├── docs/           ← ARCHITECTURE.md, DEVELOPMENT.md, CHECKLISTS.md, CHECKLISTS_ARCHIVE.md, CREATING_SKILLS.md, DESIGN.md, DEPLOYMENT.md
│   └── prompts/        ← SYSTEM.md, SAFETY.md, CONSCIOUSNESS.md
├── data/
│   ├── settings.json   ← user settings (API keys, models, budget)
│   ├── task_results/
│   │   ├── artifacts/<task_id>/   ← canonical artifacts + .artifact_manifest.json (private provenance) + .scratch_manifest.json (declared scratch fingerprints)
│   │   └── artifact_versions/<task_id>/ ← recovery history for overwritten deliverables (last 5 per name)
│   ├── task_drives/<task_id>/ ← task-scoped scratch; startup prunes terminal tasks after retention
│   ├── task_trees/<root>/blackboard.jsonl ← task-tree coordination ledger (ephemeral)
│   ├── state/
│   │   ├── state.json  ← runtime state + compatibility cost projection (never the monetary authority)
│   │   ├── usage_attempts.jsonl ← append-only monetary authority; every physical send has attempt id + lifecycle (settled cost=None + numeric reservation = unresolved; zero-usage body-error = $0)
│   │   ├── usage_attempts.quarantine.jsonl ← quarantine evidence for a corrupt final row
│   │   ├── usage_import_watermark.json ← resumable legacy-import watermark + source hashes
│   │   ├── request_wire_compatibility.json ← cross-process locked 14-day exact-route evidence; malformed fails open
│   │   ├── server_port / server_process.json ← active port; launcher-owned server identity record
│   │   ├── advisory_review.json ← durable advisory/review ledger
│   │   ├── deep_self_review_context.json ← last deep self-review Atlas manifest + model metadata
│   │   ├── code_intel/<repo_key>/inventory.json ← Code Inventory v2 facts
│   │   ├── evolution_metrics_cache.json / evolution_campaign.json / evolution_checkpoints.jsonl
│   │   ├── post_task_evolution_request.json ← promotion signal (worker-written; supervisor idle tick consumes; DROPPED when owner-stop sentinel set)
│   │   ├── post_task_evolution_counter.json ← per-drive counter for the every_n cadence
│   │   ├── scheduled_tasks.json ← queue-backed schedules (cron+tz or one-shot)
│   │   ├── claudexor_rotation_provisioning.json ← last rotation-reconcile POST receipt
│   │   ├── projects.json ← project registry (immutable id/chat, working-folder, lifecycle/tombstone, routing fence, revision); tombstones durable, never pruned
│   │   ├── project_task_bindings.json ← task→project bindings (schema v1, REQUIRED typed origin: source-row ref + source_text, or closed origin_absent reason; one-way enrichment only; one root per Project max; tombstone never removes binding)
│   │   ├── ui_preferences.json ← owner-local layout prefs + monotonic project_seen_revision ACKs
│   │   ├── queue_snapshot.json ← PENDING/RUNNING recovery projection + worker/reaping/idle counts + worker_pool_disabled_reason
│   │   ├── cancel_intents.json ← compact locked projection of ACTIVE cancel intents
│   │   ├── terminal_deliveries.json ← terminal-answer delivery registry (dedupe + PENDING outbox; drop shapes disclosed)
│   │   ├── extension_companions.json / extension_reconcile/ ← companion snapshot + reconcile markers
│   │   ├── review_continuations/ (+ archived/ + corrupt/) ← blocked-review continuation payloads; archived never deleted
│   │   ├── workspace_executor_processes/ ← executor cleanup records for panic/shutdown
│   │   ├── consciousness_observations.jsonl ← BG consciousness inbox; enqueue rows kept until settled ACK; malformed stay visible as gaps
│   │   ├── cx/ ← managed Claudexor runtime store (versioned trees, node/, cache/, install.lock)
│   │   └── skills/<skill_name>/ ← enabled.json, review.json (content-hash bound), owner_attestation.json, review_history.jsonl, accepted_rebuttals.json, deps.json, auto_repair.json, ouroboroshub.json, health.json, auth_token.json, extension_calls/, __extension_imports/<pid>-<uuid>/skill/
│   ├── claudexor/ ← owned Claudexor home (daemon descriptor/token, credential profiles, runs, ouroboros-owned.json, daemon.log) — never the operator's ~/.claudexor
│   ├── memory/
│   │   ├── identity.md / scratchpad.md / scratchpad_blocks.json / dialogue_blocks.json / dialogue_summary.md / dialogue_meta.json / WORLD.md / knowledge/ / identity_journal.jsonl / scratchpad_journal.jsonl / knowledge_journal.jsonl / knowledge_history.jsonl / knowledge/patterns_history.jsonl / deep_review.md / registry.md
│   ├── projects/<project_id>/knowledge/ ← per-project facts, isolated from memory/knowledge; projects/<id>/logs/ holds full project reflections
│   ├── observability/
│   │   ├── blobs/<sha256>.json.gz ← private compressed forensic payloads
│   │   └── calls/<task_id>/<call_id>.json ← private call manifests (blob refs, hashes, timing, usage)
│   ├── services/<task_id>/<service>.log ← task-scoped service logs (bounded redacted tails + blob refs)
│   ├── logs/
│   │   ├── chat.jsonl ← canonical chat log (one logical message stored once)
│   │   ├── chat_annotations.jsonl ← compact routing status by client_message_id
│   │   ├── progress.jsonl / events.jsonl / tools.jsonl / supervisor.jsonl ← execution traces
│   │   ├── task_reflections.jsonl ← execution reflections (project roots leave a bounded pointer row)
│   │   ├── containment_faults.jsonl ← delegated-run containment incidents (compact projection)
│   │   └── skills/         ← optional skill/companion runtime logs
│   ├── archive/            ← rotated logs, rescue snapshots
│   └── uploads/            ← chat file attachments
├── Deliverables/      ← visible user-deliverables container (BARE user_files filename without a directory lands here; OUROBOROS_DELIVERABLES_ROOT; sibling of projects/, outside repo/ and data/, never GC-pruned)
└── ouroboros.pid           ← PID lock file (auto-released on crash)
```
