For a scheduled configured nanny, `subagent_bootstrap.bootstrap_before_context` freezes the route, compiles the complete work order, and — after recovery adoption and the durable zero-run/unknown-evidence fences — STARTS the exact snapshotted leaf before the first model round through the same `delegate_start(prompt="")` wrapper. The first turn arrives with the live run's startup receipt for judgment: supervise with `delegate_wait`, schedule auxiliary children, publish evidence, or — when the leaf could not start and absence is proven — record a typed zero-run decision. Root-direct bounded work and same-nanny replacement use the same `exact_start` primitive. The physical leaf is never started from a host fallback or coordination prefix. `delegate_start(subagent_id=..., prompt=..., root="skill_payload", bucket=..., skill_name=...)` is the orthogonal exact-resource selector: id chooses transport, binding chooses payload authority.

**Work-order compiler.** One total 250,000-character wire budget (an Ouroboros serializer integrity bound). Fitting orders are byte-complete. Larger orders: the host records the full-order SHA/size and starts only a route whose live manifest declares an interactive question channel with a compact coverage=partial source-request lens; the actor asks for exact source ranges and the nanny answers via waiting_on_user/delegate_answer from the canonical-work-order projection of `get_task_result`. The manifest observation is a point-in-time preflight, not a lease; only durable verified coverage authorizes completion, so a raced run remains `cannot_verify` and patch application stays refused. An unavailable channel returns a typed source-channel refusal before POST. Crash recovery replays the durable compact body and full-order fingerprint. `delegate_answer(source_response=...)` verifies the canonical selector, full digest, range bounds, and exact rendered bytes before appending a receipt; a retry receiving `already_resolved` may repair a lost receipt only when the durable receipt proves the same interaction and exact bytes previously returned `delivered`; a timeout or unrelated resolution remains `cannot_verify`. A terminal run whose intervals do not cover the brief is typed `cannot_verify`; the integration seam refuses apply.

**Live progress** uses Claudexor's additive `textKind`/`textDelta` facts to join adjacent fragments of the same kind, attempt, and harness without inserting punctuation or changing whitespace; complete messages, tools, statuses, and legacy rows retain event boundaries.

**Event-only sleep.** `delegate_wait` is model-visible as an event-only sleep, not a caller-sized poll. `delegate_supervision.supervised_wait` renews low-level transport windows in host code; journal advances stream to the human but do not return to the model. A meaningful terminal settlement, new interaction, containment/transport/custody fault, addressed owner/task message, direct-child beacon or terminal transition, cancel/deadline control, or recovery judgment becomes a coalesced durable pending wake — injected and acknowledged once, replayed after interruption until acknowledged. An oversized wake returns valid bounded JSON plus a hash-verified source reference; failure to stage it leaves the wake pending for replay. Child signals filter to the current task's direct lineage. Quiet renewal makes zero LLM calls; the external-wait lease protects silence from the idle rail, while deadline, absolute ceiling, budget, and cancellation remain outer bounds.

**Main-loop cognition in-flight fact.** Immediately before each exact provider-call seam the worker sends a supervisor `started` event bound to task attempt/execution/round/call id/retry attempt; every success, empty or failed response, and accounting failure sends the matching terminal fact. The supervisor keeps only that process-local active row in `RUNNING` metadata, no elapsed-time expiry, consulted only by the idle predicate: `OUROBOROS_LLM_TRANSPORT_READ_TIMEOUT_SEC` (2700s default) is a configurable dead-socket bound, not a cognition deadline; explicit deadline, budget, cancellation, and absolute ceiling remain independent hard axes. A stale terminal from an earlier retry cannot clear the current row.

**Keepalive transport.** Cached OpenAI-compatible, no-proxy per-call, and web-search clients share one factory (`net_transport.py`) setting platform-guarded TCP keepalive socket options so a dropped NAT/VPN mapping is detected by kernel probes instead of hanging to the read timeout (Linux/macOS within minutes; others `SO_KEEPALIVE`). Cached clients carry SDK-equivalent pool limits. When a proxy httpx would honor is configured, cached and web-search clients skip the explicit transport — disclosed residual: proxy-routed installs and httpx builds predating `socket_options` run without keepalive tuning; likewise the native Anthropic `requests` session, the anthropic web-search client, the GigaChat library client, and `llm_probe` ephemeral clients.

**Coordination context.** The startup/recovery receipt and every newly minted meaningful wake carry one host-rendered `coordination_context`: the complete parent-authored advisory `delegation_budget.intent_note`, explicit-deadline time remaining, known/partial/unknown root-tree spend, active host-visible descendants, and the canonical root's remaining paid acceptance capacity; vendor-internal descendants are explicitly opaque. Facts for LLM judgment, not thresholds; replay returns the stored snapshot byte-for-byte while the next acknowledged wake recomputes it. Oversized wake → complete context stays in the wake source, bounded envelope carries a typed source-only projection. Active descendants come only from a fresh queue snapshot plus targeted parent chains.

**Checkpoint.** The nanny may request one future inspection with `checkpoint_after_sec` and a free-text `checkpoint_reason`; it wakes once at that time or an earlier real event consumes it; every later inspection needs a fresh model decision — no repeating cadence, journal-progress wake, stall classifier, or hidden polling loop. On a wake the nanny retains its full normal tool surface and inherited parent model/effort route; its role is to inspect, coordinate, answer, wait, cancel/replace, evaluate, integrate, or create a different child — never to implement the same healthy leaf's assignment in parallel.

**Recovery exact and cause-specific.** A proven non-signal worker crash may reserve the same task id as a recoverable successor before requeue; the custody orphan sweep receives that narrow fence, and the successor adopts the exact run or pending invocation before any LLM call. A planned agent self-restart first persists `delegate_restart_transaction_prepared` for each sleeping/wake-pending nanny (config/work-order/authority/worktree/run or pending-invocation/cursor/message/interaction/checkpoint fingerprints). The launcher acknowledges the expected exit-42 transaction; startup pre-adopts those handoffs before ordinary custody cleanup; adoption is durably recorded before supervision continues, and a terminal observed in the gap settles once.

A configured-session physical start proves its actor/work order against the task-start snapshot. For a root-direct bounded start, or same-nanny replacement after the predecessor is terminal and any captured physical result is disposed, the one current durable custody holder is the immutable actor/config/work-order authority; task authority and the holder's worktree/run binding are re-proved, zero or multiple holders refuse, and recovery never reselects from mutable settings. Owner restart, panic, external or worker signal, deadline/timeout, explicit cancellation, and abrupt whole-app loss are explicit no-resume causes — they cancel/settle. Any missing, conflicting, vetoed, or ambiguous binding returns typed recovery-required/reconciliation and never starts a duplicate mutator; no public `PARKED` state — the task remains ordinary `RUNNING` while sleeping. Key supervision telemetry: `delegate_supervision_wait_entered`, `delegate_supervision_wait_renewed`, `delegate_supervision_wake_pending`, `delegate_supervision_wake_replayed`, `delegate_supervision_wake_acknowledged`, `delegate_supervision_checkpoint_scheduled`, `delegate_supervision_checkpoint_consumed`; recovery emits `delegate_restart_transaction_prepared`, `delegate_restart_transaction_acknowledged`, `delegate_recovery_pre_adopted`, `delegate_run_adopted`.

**A run's question is the nanny's to answer.** `pendingInteractions` — full question text, header, options, `multi_select` — is read by the ONE normalizer `gateways.claudexor.pending_interactions`; supervision wakes IMMEDIATELY with a typed `status="waiting_on_user"` payload on a NEW interaction instead of burning the engine's answer timeout in metered polling. An oversized question set spills WHOLE to `task_drive` with a sha256/size receipt and a counted bounded preview inline; `interaction_id`/`question_id` ride WHOLE (echoed into `delegate_answer`). Delivered interaction ids are recorded durably and acknowledged only after transcript injection. The engine's interaction timeout (benign decline) is the backstop only for a question that CARRIES a `timeout_at`; null means no expiry — the run waits. `delegate_answer` is custody-gated and relays the engine's OWN typed outcomes (`delivered`/`already_resolved`/`not_found`/`rejected` — the last only for payload-semantic 4xx: 400/409/413/422); a spent subscription window is the distinct `subscription_window_exhausted` outcome carrying `reset_at`; ambiguous transport is `delivery_unknown` with a detail re-read; a different answer is never auto-retried. Policy: answer from held task context; a question above authority (money, scope, external actions) rides the escalation verb to the nearest LIVE ancestor while the nanny keeps waiting. Hosted REVIEW slots are non-interactive by contract: their poller waits out a parked question only when its engine expiry provably lands before the slot deadline (benign decline); otherwise the slot terminates early and typed (`review_session_waiting_on_user`, cancelled through the verified-cancel path, "host-cancelled" only on a verified receipt; a verify read finding the run already succeeded consumes that terminal as the slot's ordinary result). The codex lane has no mid-run channel: a terminal with `outcome_facts.reason=input_required` is answered by a plain NEW `delegate_start(subagent_id=..., prompt=...)` carrying the assignment plus the answers — never the engine's rerun/decision verb (that would start a run outside this task's custody trail). `delegate_answer` is deliberately absent from the configured bootstrap's required tool set: a nanny without it is degraded (a `timeout_at`-bearing question benign-declines; one without waits), never custody-broken.

**Configured dispatch is exact.** `subagent_runtime.resolve_configured_actor_dispatch` reads only the immutable task snapshot. An API row resolves to the exact API model/effort and an ordinary recursive child; a session row re-checks the exact saved route and resolves to a nanny on the parent's captured cognitive model/effort (never forced to Light). Disabled/malformed/unknown/unavailable choices return a typed reason, current alternatives, and any known reset time with `host_fallback: false`; the host never ranks alternatives, changes session work to API/native work, or picks the first healthy row. Claudexor may rotate compatible accounts inside one unpinned route — credential transport, not a different actor choice.

The child's execution substrate was decided by its PARENT: selecting an `agent_session` row IS the typed LLM decision that this work executes on the harness, so the host executes that choice by construction and the nanny never re-decides it. The typed parent choice is the FLOOR the host hardcodes — truth, money, authorship — while topology, decomposition, and supervision judgment remain the model's CEILING (BIBLE P13/P5). Bootstrap starts the exact snapshotted leaf BEFORE the first metered round through the same `delegate_start(prompt="")` wrapper — one start path, one set of refusal shapes — after recovery adoption (first) and the durable zero-run/unknown-evidence fences (second: a fence may hide a live prior run, so a fence-wake outranks every terminal). The host never waits inside bootstrap: a live run hands the model its first round immediately with a `configured_session_started` receipt carrying the run id; waiting is the model's own `delegate_wait` decision, so owner messages, hurry controls, loop checkpoints, and parallel auxiliary children stay live. A blocked dispatch or DEFINITE start refusal — typed, no custody handle, reason in the closed `subagent_bootstrap._DEFINITE_UNRUN_REASONS` set or the access-profile mismatch family — ends the child UNRUN and typed at $0 through the existing `executor_blocked_outcome` (`agent.py` fills `cap_info` from `ctx._configured_startup_refusal`); everything ambiguous — any custody handle, `started_uncustodied`, unknown codes, unparseable output — wakes the model, because a false "spent nothing" terminal over a possibly-live run is the one direction classification must never fail toward.

**Zero-run receipt.** When no physical run exists and none can be started, the model may finish with a typed zero-run receipt through `verify_and_record` carrying `zero_run_decision` (`incomplete` or `unknown` — the WRITE enum, `ZERO_RUN_WRITE_DECISIONS`; the READ enum keeps historical `complete` rows valid so old receipts still fence a second start, while the terminal projection degrades them to `unknown` + disclosure, reason `historical_zero_run_complete`, never clean) and a `zero_run_basis`. The canonical custody root must first prove no open run, ambiguous start invocation, or undisposed physical result remains. Once durably recorded the decision is terminal for that actor; a later physical start is refused. Malformed/unreadable receipt store → authority typed unknown, physical start refused; the narrow zero-run tool can re-ground it; child copy-back never launders the corrupt source through a whole-file rewrite. A valid terminal zero-run row still wins over unrelated malformed rows. (`contract_kind="delegation_zero_run"` records the terminal.)

**Clean terminal.** A session actor's terminal is CLEAN only through its own physical leaf (a SUCCEEDED run or an adoption — a start merely accepted projects incomplete/unknown) or a durable typed zero-run receipt. "Completed direct child ⇒ clean" is deleted: host children are auxiliary evidence, and the unresolved fact (`physical_leaf_not_started`, with `direct_child_statuses` carried alongside) rides the terminal projection as an incomplete/unknown execution axis. This remains an LLM-first affordance, not a host topology/state machine: host code executes the parent's typed substrate choice and reports the truth. The canonical work order remains byte-complete and hash-bound; any coordination context is additive and separately disclosed; an appendix over the instruction-field budget refuses before provisioning.

**Startup custody fault.** `started_uncustodied` is a startup custody fault, not a healthy third state: the exact run/invocation is surfaced, quiet supervision does not begin, and no replacement may start until the original run is proven absent or terminal and any captured physical result is explicitly disposed. Recovery replays the stored canonical request and idempotency key rather than issuing a second semantic start.

**Route health.** The public `delegate_start` and the configured actor bridge share the same exact route-health and start primitive. A retry must replay the already-bound invocation; a scheduled nanny uses its task snapshot; root-direct or same-nanny replacement supplies an explicit session `subagent_id`. No path chooses a first/healthy alternative. Tool visibility is checked as part of configured bootstrap: a selected session whose required custody verbs are unavailable returns a typed startup refusal; never the historical preflight-native fallback or a second lane/model resolution.

`subagents.route_health` is the ONE health reader for ALL consumers — the dispatcher, the nanny's own `delegate_start`, and the review slots (`review_execution`, `plan_review_runtime`) — and it does not guess admission. The harness row's aggregate doctor `status` is NOT a refusal: it describes the DEFAULT credential store while real accounts live in the engine's credential-profile pool. Admission belongs to the engine: an empty/exhausted pool answers the start POST with its own typed refusal (`credential_pool_exhausted` + earliest reset), $0 and zero model rounds. The row's `enabled` field is honored for unpinned routes as `route_disabled` (the engine schema defines it as the OWNER's toggle, not an observation; a pinned profile keeps its historical skip). The belt capability row is not consulted: Ouroboros runs never request the belt. Remaining structural refusals: `route_not_in_capability_catalog`, `route_disabled` (unpinned), an access-profile mismatch, `engine_rejects_delegated_marker`, and positive quota exhaustion for the route's own model. Review slots inherit "the engine decides": a degraded-status reviewer slot reaches the engine and gets its typed refusal — never a silent fallback onto metered api spend. On the `auto` lane, a dead DAEMON (`ClaudexorUnavailable`) and an owner-disabled harness keep the native fallback with its visible marker, while "daemon alive, pool empty" is discovered at the engine — the dispatched nanny learns the typed refusal there and does the work natively with disclosure.

**Model-visible selection is a bounded semi-stable catalog.** `subagent_runtime.model_visible_subagent_catalog(settings)` projects every saved row in owner order, verbatim description included: stable id/name, route class, requested model or session target, requested effort, automatic-versus-pinned account policy, config fingerprint, source, LLM-first selection guidance, and the exact-start-or-typed-refusal dispatch contract. Invalid, undecided/unsaved, disabled or empty configuration projects nothing. `current_model_visible_subagent_catalog()` reads the current saved settings plus the task-start-normalized legacy overlay; `context._capture_context_core` serializes the ordered JSON under `## Available subagents` in the semi-stable context block before memory/knowledge. No fresh Claudexor probe and no host ranking on each round. Dispatch/start remains the authoritative live check. A failure building either projection drops only that fact.

**Nanny economics are structurally quiet.** The physical leaf is already live when the nanny's first metered round arrives, so those rounds exist for judgment: the burn baseline resets ONLY on real acts of delegation (`delegate_start` / `schedule_subagent`), supervision verbs (`delegate_wait`/`delegate_answer`/`delegate_cancel`) advance the baseline while dollars accumulate, and coordination verbs never buy metered silence (`nanny_pacing`). The machinery arms whenever the harness was requested — `_nanny_route_dispatched` = a configured `agent_session` row OR `executor == "harness"`. While the leaf is healthy, supervision performs zero LLM calls regardless of journal activity. On a real wake the nanny uses the parent's captured model and effort, so difficult acceptance and recovery are not forced onto Light. Metered calls, overlapping tool work, descendants and delegated spend all remain visible in the ordinary usage/custody receipts.

At completion, durable evidence separates selected intent from execution fact. `actual_substrate` (`harness_used` / `harness_attempted` / `native_only`) derives only from custody rows; unreadable evidence yields no zero-count/native claim. Requested and effective harness/model/account/access, start/settlement states, disclosed or unknown spend, patch disposition and output-read completion remain queryable. A typed startup refusal or selected route failure does not authorize productive work on a different substrate. Another session route is an explicit exact start; API fallback is an explicit separately visible recursive child selected by an LLM.

**Read-only and mutating session rows share one nanny transport.** The only difference is the run shape, and the shape has ONE owner — `subagents.delegated_run_shape`. For the ordinary workspace path it answers one question — is this an acting child? — asked of the live `ToolContext` by `tools/delegate._derive_authority` (`tool_access.active_tool_profile`) and of the configured task snapshot by `subagent_runtime.resolve_configured_actor_dispatch` (`tool_access.predicted_subagent_profile`); neither reassembles the shape. The EXACT-RESOURCE lane is the second explicit entry: `delegate_start(subagent_id=..., prompt=..., root="skill_payload", bucket=..., skill_name=...)` selects one installed user-managed skill payload, including a physical native payload without `.seed-origin` through its logical `external` source, authorized through a fresh `ResolvedResourceBinding` for `skill_payload.write` (top-level task profiles only) rather than through the acting-child question.

| task authority | access | mode | isolation | `execution.delegated` |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| acting subagent (valid write surface) | `workspace_write` | `agent` | `live` | `true` |
| ROOT of an external-workspace task (validated active workspace) | `workspace_write` | `agent` | `live` | `true` |
| top-level task selecting an exact skill payload (`root="skill_payload"`) | `workspace_write` | `agent` | `live` | `true` |
| anything else, including a fail-closed subagent | `readonly` | `ask` | envelope (default) | not sent |

WHERE changes are destined and how they travel is a separate record — the unified host-derived **mutation authority** (`tools/delegate._mutation_authority` / `_payload_mutation_authority`), never model-supplied:

| source | `target_root` derivation | `capture_mode` |
|---|---|---|
| `acting_constraint` | the child's own `task_constraint.write_root`, required to equal the genuinely ACTIVE workspace root | `delegated_snapshot` |
| `external_workspace_root` | the root task's validated active external workspace (a root holds no acting constraint — it already holds write+shell inside the project; the snapshot + explicit-apply record supplies per-run provenance) | `delegated_snapshot` |
| `skill_payload` | the exact payload the fresh `skill_payload.write` binding resolved; the durable record also carries the semantic `resource_ref` (source/skill_name/CAS baseline hash) that retry and apply re-resolve | `delegated_snapshot` |
| `readonly` | ordinary active root (nothing to write) | `none` |

**Payload snapshot.** A payload target gets a STANDALONE private Git snapshot (`subagent_worktrees.provision_payload_snapshot`): the live payload is never initialized as Git; the loader-visible inventory is copied out, committed as a synthetic baseline whose identity is durably recorded, and the run is scoped there. Capture trusts NOTHING under the child-writable snapshot's `.git`: symlinked Git metadata is a typed refusal, and the diff is built in a parent-owned control GIT_DIR with a fresh temp index seeded from the recorded baseline commit (child `.git/index` and `.git/config` are never read or written). The baseline and capture stage RAW bytes (`stage_raw_payload_inventory`: `hash-object --no-filters` + `--index-info`, so a `.gitattributes` eol/clean filter is inert); regular modes pinned to baseline/100644; a non-empty patch whose result loader hash equals the baseline is a typed `unreviewable_metadata_change` refusal; after a real apply the live loader hash must equal the recorded result hash or the run fails typed with its apply intent PENDING while the stale-extension reconcile marker stays queued. At disposition the apply is a LIVE, index-free `git apply` into the non-Git payload guarded by a whole-payload content-hash CAS (drift = typed conflict; identical content = idempotent applied), with reserved lifecycle/control paths and escaping-symlink candidates refusing the WHOLE apply; a successful apply QUEUES the extension reconcile request and the skill's existing review becomes STALE for the new content hash.

**A mutating run executes in a PRIVATE EXECUTION SNAPSHOT, never in the shared tree** (metered children keep sharing; only delegated runs are isolated). At `delegate_start` the host snapshots the target's REAL current state — tracked + staged + eligible untracked, with the same sensitive/credential veto the workspace-patch capture applies, DECIDED BEFORE ANYTHING IS HASHED — into a synthetic baseline commit pinned by a `refs/ouroboros/delegated/` ref, and checks out a detached worktree (`subagent_worktrees.provision_execution_snapshot`). On Claudexor `3.8.1+`, `scope.root` remains the stable authority target while `execution.workspaceRoot` names the one-shot snapshot; the strict `3.8.0` wire stays byte-compatible by using the snapshot as `scope.root`. The typed binding `{target_root, execution_root, baseline_sha, authority_source, snapshot_id}` is recorded durably BEFORE the POST. An explicit retry reproduces it exactly (a GC-collected snapshot is a typed `execution_snapshot_missing` refusal, never a re-mint; a stored pre-isolation mutating invocation is the typed `retry_binding_absent` refusal). The run still runs `live` — in place FROM THE ENGINE'S view — so the scoped-HOME/`delegated` marker still applies.

**Apply/reject is explicit.** At terminal, `delegate_wait` captures the run's diff against the baseline durably and reports it as the `workspace_capture` block; NOTHING reaches the target automatically — the nanny EXPLICITLY applies or rejects through `integrate_delegated_patch`. That orchestration — capture, explicit disposition, durable rows, snapshot custody — is UNIVERSAL across both lanes. What differs is the staging substrate. A GIT workspace target captures through `write_workspace_patch_artifacts` (sensitive veto, binary/mode handling, sha256 manifest) and applies under the repo git lock: it PROVES first that no touched path drifted from `baseline_sha`, then applies to the working tree and STAGES the existing-or-indexed paths, writes a verdict artifact and a durable disposition row — staged, never committed. A SKILL-PAYLOAD target captures through the payload adapter (`_write_payload_patch_artifacts`) and applies LIVE into the non-Git payload under the whole-payload content-hash CAS. Touched paths are read NUL-safely from `git apply --numstat -z` in BOTH directions. The Ouroboros protected-path gate applies only when the target IS the Ouroboros body. Review sees exactly the run's own diff; receipts carry real per-run authorship. A conflict (proven drift) is owned by the nanny: snapshot and captured patch persist until explicit resolution or discard. CLEANUP FOLLOWS THE DURABLE ROW: an unwritten disposition returns typed `INTEGRATE_DISPOSITION_UNWRITTEN`; a successful apply whose staging failed returns `INTEGRATE_APPLIED_UNSTAGED`. Mutation rides an APPLY-INTENT PROTOCOL: a durable `delegate_run_patch_apply_started` row is written after protected-path checks and before any tree mutation; every provably-non-mutating outcome resolves it with `delegate_run_patch_apply_resolved`; an intent row that cannot be written refuses to mutate (`INTEGRATE_INTENT_UNWRITTEN`). On replay, a pending intent without a disposition means the tree MAY already carry the patch: both decisions answer `INTEGRATE_DELEGATED_APPLY_AMBIGUOUS`. Explicit owner exit: re-run `integrate_delegated_patch` with `acknowledge_ambiguous=true` after inspecting — the pending intent is durably resolved as `owner_acknowledged` and the NORMAL guards re-run from scratch; the flag is a no-op when nothing is pending. Split-drive visibility: `artifacts.delegated_capture_read_target` narrowly rebinds `artifact_store` READ operations for the owning task's own `delegated_runs/` prefix to the canonical root. Startup GC removes only snapshots custody proves closed, cross-checking open runs AND pending invocations. A read-only child stays in Claudexor's default envelope — `execution.isolation='live'` is agent-only and a non-agent run carrying it is refused at the boundary — one transport with one derived difference, not a second pipeline.

**Terminal reconciliation captures only over PROVEN terminality; `patch_captured` means "a usable artifact exists".** When the OWNER task is gone (orphan sweep, kill-path reconcile, in-process release), a settled mutating run's diff is captured through `delegate_integration.capture_terminal_patch_for_drive` — capture only, never an apply: the apply/reject DECISION stays with an owner, and the obligation is visible on the health surface (`delegate_custody.undisposed_patches` → "DELEGATED PATCH AWAITS DISPOSITION") until the durable `PATCH_DISPOSED` row clears it. Pending-invocation recovery carries the FULL snapshot binding into the recovered STARTED row, so startup GC never deletes the snapshot holding the child's only work. Capture is EAGER only where a terminal receipt PROVES the run over (the `is_terminal(detail)` settle path, and a cancel whose read-back verified terminal); a run closed on the ABSENT branch (daemon 404) or left UNREADABLE captures NOTHING — the child may still be alive and writing. The snapshot stays preserved; the health line words the truth ("changes captured" only when `patch_captured`; otherwise "work preserved … captured at disposition"); `integrate_delegated_patch` performs capture-on-demand before applying/rejecting — disposition is the retry point for a failed capture. A capture that fails at disposition returns typed `INTEGRATE_DELEGATED_CAPTURE_FAILED` for BOTH decisions; no disposition is recorded, the obligation stays open. The capture core mints `patch_captured` only over a manifest whose OWN status is ready (`ready_with_changes` / `ready_no_changes`); a manifest reporting its own failure leaves the row uncaptured; the reject branch re-checks the manifest before releasing the snapshot.

**The stored `delegated_runs_unreconciled` projection is healed only from the write side, at three seams.** Readers serve the stored projection (projection-over-replay, no live custody join), so a run settled AFTER the task's terminal write stays stale until a write-side refresh: the periodic sweep refreshes the tasks named in its own reconcile outcomes; the boot backfill (`delegate_terminal.backfill_terminal_reconciliations`, once per server generation, after the startup orphan reconcile) re-audits every stored TERMINAL row still disclosing unreconciled runs under one shared custody snapshot; and the kill paths clear a stale list — running-kill cancel and reaper terminal/retry writes carry the fresh audit UNCONDITIONALLY, while the fast already-settled kill lane runs the same guarded refresh (`trigger=kill_path_clear`). Finalize-on-miss: the newly-cancelled branch stores the audited list AND the reconciliation envelope in its one terminal write; the already-settled branch runs the guarded `kill_path_clear` refresh. Every refresh is audit-only (never cancels), never rewrites `reason_code`, and never recomputes the frozen `delegated_runs_started/settled/succeeded/failed` counters — those remain a HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT; current liveness lives in the `delegate_terminal_reconciliation` envelope (`trigger` — `sweep_refresh`/`boot_backfill`/`kill_path_clear`, other recorder callers stamp their own (e.g. `loop_exit`) — plus `open_run_ids`/`pending_invocation_ids`/`undisposed_patch_run_ids`). An audit that MATCHES the stored disclosure writes nothing and emits no custody event; patch debt always survives a refresh as `patch:<run_id>`, never a blind clear.

**Delegated-isolation residuals (disclosed):** disposition requires the OWNING task identity (`integrate_delegated_patch` refuses FOREIGN), so an orphan's captured patch is disclosed and preserved but a fresh task cannot apply it; a run whose snapshot was GC-lost or whose capture keeps failing can never satisfy its obligation — the typed refusal is deliberate; an UNDISPOSED snapshot persists until explicit disposition; the baseline is WORKTREE-PRIMARY (`git update-index --add --remove` stages current worktree content); a crash BETWEEN `git worktree add` and the registry write leaves an unregistered checkout plus its `refs/ouroboros/delegated/` ref that no GC sees (recovered only by idempotent re-provision of the SAME snapshot id or by hand); the git lock is TASK-DRIVE scoped, so two nannies integrating into the SAME external tree can interleave apply+stage sequences — real only in a multi-nanny swarm, the drift check makes the loser a typed conflict; a credential-shaped file the CHILD creates inside its snapshot is vetoed and fails the whole patch. The root external-workspace lane holds unit-level authority tests. `prompts/SYSTEM.md` deliberately teaches the `integrate_delegated_patch` flow in-context (tool description, started-note, `workspace_capture` block).

**A mutating run asks for containment, reads back what it got, and DISCLOSES the gap instead of refusing the work.** In place is the one shape where Claudexor otherwise hands the harness the operator's REAL `$HOME` (which holds the daemon token — a bearer for the entire `/v2` control API). Four things follow, one mechanism:

- **The marker travels with the isolation.** `execution.delegated: true` rides in the same record as `isolation: live`, built from `delegated_run_shape` in one place.
- **The version floor is about the SCHEMA.** `config.CLAUDEXOR_DELEGATED_MARKER_MIN_VERSION` (3.3.0) is the oldest engine whose `RunExecution` accepts `delegated` at all; below it the start is a 400 and no run exists. Checked inside `subagents.route_health`, so the DISPATCHER refuses before a token is spent and the nanny's own `delegate_start` gives the identical typed `engine_rejects_delegated_marker` blocker. It has to be a version and not a capability probe: `RunExecution` is STRICT (an unknown key is a 400) and the catalog's `runControlKeys` are TOP-LEVEL request keys only, so a nested marker is undiscoverable. READ-ONLY delegation sends no marker and keeps the lower transport floor (`CLAUDEXOR_MIN_VERSION` = 3.2.0). THE TWO FLOORS ARE DIFFERENT NUMBERS ON PURPOSE: an engine between them serves read-only and refuses mutating. The floor is NOT a proxy for "a boundary was applied": Claudexor's boundary is macOS-only (`docs/DELEGATED_CONFINEMENT.md` §8) and a build declares the same number on every host. Threat model and non-coverage: `docs/DELEGATED_ADMISSION.md`.
- **What was APPLIED is asked of the attempt, never of the OS.** Claudexor records the applied facts — `harness_home_isolated` / `harness_home_dir`, and the boundary as `confinement_mechanism` plus the `confinement_verified_denied_path` it was proven against — on `attempts/<id>/attempt.yaml`; the boundary is also on the run detail as `candidates[].confinement` (since 3.3.6). `delegate_wait` reads the artifact, answering both halves at once (`gateways.claudexor.attempt_containment`). The mechanism is an OPAQUE string; `sys.platform` appears nowhere in the decision.
- **A missing boundary is disclosed in three places, not refused.** A run that recorded no mechanism still runs; the fact reaches the durable event stream (`delegate_run_unconfined`), the child's own instructions (its boundary is stated as a request, so it does not describe itself as sandboxed), and the parent's terminal payload (`containment` carries `os_boundary`, `verified`, `disclosed`/`attempts`) — "Disclose instead of forbid". A recorded FALSE is still a fault — `harness_home_isolated: false`, or a scoped home that IS the operator's own, is cancelled as a typed containment fault, exactly like a widened access profile. A scoped home NESTED under `$HOME` — with or without a recorded boundary — is the engine's own layout on boundary-less hosts (every non-macOS host today) and flows to this disclosed-unconfined path. The engine's typed `confinement_unavailable_reason` rides the disclosure as telemetry, never as an admission token. A MISSING home fact is neither: the engine writes two attempt records and only the clean one carries those fields. Unproven is REPORTED, so silence is never read as success and never enforced as a breach.

**The model cannot widen its own authority.** `delegate_start` exposes `prompt`, exact session `subagent_id`, `max_seconds`, recovery-only `retry_of`, and the exact-resource selector (`root="skill_payload"` + `bucket` + `skill_name`). The retry may replay only the same task's stored canonical body under its original idempotency key; prompt or ownership mismatch is rejected; it cannot change route, root, access, or permissions. There is no access, mode, isolation, or scope argument — the selector NAMES a resource rather than granting one. Every delegated run carries host-authored `instructions` stating the same prohibitions an ordinary subagent has — no commit or history move, no self-review, no runtime controls, skills or memory, no writes outside the root (a payload run's variant states that editing THAT skill's user-authored files in the private copy IS the assignment) — plus the hosting task's own contract objective/expected_output (host-read, host-authored). Those are a statement; the enforcement is the access profile plus the patch capture. Because Claudexor DERIVES effective access rather than echoing the request, `delegate_wait` verifies rather than assumes: every fetched run detail goes through `_containment_breach`, the one reader for BOTH halves of containment — the access profile and the harness HOME. A run enforced WIDER than the task is entitled to is cancelled and returned as a typed `access_profile_widened` refusal; a narrower effective profile is fine — the engine itself clamps `workspace_write` down to `readonly` on an `ask` run.

**Harness-agnostic by construction.** An `agent_session` row holds an opaque Claudexor target (`harness` or `harness=model`) plus optional credential pin and effort. Health comes from the published manifest/catalog/quota surfaces; Ouroboros asks for an access profile (`readonly` / `workspace_write`) derived from task authority and lets Claudexor choose the harness-specific mechanism. No harness-name branch selects a capability or fallback in core dispatch; the small login-wire asymmetries remain presentation/control adapters in `gateway/claudexor_accounts.py`, not task-routing policy.

**Routes and legacy keys.** `OUROBOROS_SUBAGENTS` deliberately contains both API and session routes because it is an actor-selection setting, not a provider-model sweep. `route_spec.py` shares the neutral route/pin/effort primitive with reviewer rows while preserving their different public spellings (`api_model` + `credential_profile_id` versus `api_chat` + `profile_id`) and semantic owners. Provider credentials/base URLs stay global; the active provider-model key set excludes Heavy.

The singleton `OUROBOROS_SUBAGENT_HARNESS` / `OUROBOROS_SUBAGENT_PROFILE`, `OUROBOROS_MODEL_HEAVY`, `USE_LOCAL_HEAVY`, and live lane/executor fields are bounded migration/history inputs only. When the canonical list is absent, a parseable singleton produces a session candidate with its pin, custom Heavy may produce an explicit API row, and Light may supply the Fast scout. Literal `off` becomes `enabled=false`; absent/empty is `undecided`; malformed non-empty input fails closed. Reads do not persist the candidate. Once a valid canonical list exists it wins, is not double-written back to legacy keys, and every active task freezes its own snapshot.

**Subscription preference remains LLM-first.** Onboarding and a clean undecided Settings draft surface every real connected session actor rather than a singleton; after an exact selection the host either starts that route or reports why it could not — never a keyword router or automatic API substitution.

**Read provenance on the accounts surface.** `GET /api/claudexor/status` carries a `reads` block (`ClaudexorStatusReads`: `catalog` / `accounts` / `quota`, each `ok` | `not_read` | `failed`) because the owned daemon starts LAZILY. `ok` makes the matching collection authoritative; `not_read` means nothing was asked; `failed` means it was asked and no usable answer came back. Facets are independent. Client-side the rule lives in ONE reader, `facetReadState` in `web/modules/claudexor_status_store.js`, consumed through that module's ONE shared store (`claudexorStatus`) — the single WRITER of the client-side snapshot; a wake refusal is retired only on PROVEN recovery (a daemon that answered). The store adds dimensions the wire cannot carry: `transport` (the request itself never completed) and `unread` (this client has not read yet). A read block that is present but unusable, or a facet value this build does not know, is `failed` — never authoritative — and neither is the aggregate `daemon.state`: it reports `unreachable` for a PARTIAL refusal and `running` when a facet failed on SHAPE rather than by raising. An authenticated `running` handshake is positive evidence; a facet's own `ok` is the evidence when it is not; the aggregate is never the negative answer. Reviewer-slot pins are labelled each from ITS OWN facet — an account pin from `accounts`, a model from `catalog` — and the client's facet list is parity-tested against `ClaudexorStatusReads`. A facet is `ok` only when EVERY key its envelope promised arrived.

**Login-job custody and reconciliation.** The daemon remains the sole process/fence authority; the browser keeps only the current card's custody evidence. The frozen gateway success shape is one top-level `job` plus operation-specific metadata; `ClaudexorLoginJobProblem` carries required `error` and optional stable `code` / bounded `required_actions`. Snapshot is the daemon's canonical envelope and is not wrapped again. A terminal state proves release except when its outcome reason is `termination_unconfirmed` and no `terminationReconciliation.status=empty` exists. Reconcile is an explicit POST, never passive polling. Poll/cancel/reconcile 404/410 mean only that the browser job record is absent; input keeps its distinct 404 capability result, and only input/reconcile expose typed 409s.
### Git and commit review

`tools/git.py` owns repository writes, staging, reviewed commit, rollback/restore, tags, push, CI follow-up. `mutation_attribution.py` captures the root-task baseline and projects only the clean-at-baseline system-repo delta; a dirty pre-existing path, stale/missing baseline, or failed scan blocks staging. `commit_reviewed(paths=None)` stages only that attributed candidate (explicit paths must be a subset; empty → `GIT_NO_ATTRIBUTED_CHANGES`). Managed updates keep a separate typed whole-tree authority.

A reviewed commit binds to one staged fingerprint. The advisory pass is LLM-skippable, but its audited skip never covers tests, triad, applicable scope review, aggregation, or exact-SHA binding. The hermetic preflight runs the candidate in a disposable worktree/data root; triad and scope inspect the same snapshot; any mutation stales the binding. Managed exception: a resolution commit reviews the declared M0→S subject (`tools/review_subject.py`) — the delta between tx-pinned mechanical merge M0 and candidate S — and the gate binds S to the index write-tree the fingerprint pins. External wrappers report readiness, never commit authority.

The binding includes the `git write-tree` SHA, ordered `HEAD`/`MERGE_HEAD` parents, indexed VERSION, expected `v{VERSION}` tag, any existing tag target, and the binary staged-diff hash; tree/parents/VERSION/tag are re-read after commit before success or push is recorded. An existing release tag is never silently accepted or retargeted. Durable state keeps attempts, obligations, readiness debt, raw actor evidence, and the final binding.

`review_status(include_raw=true)` exposes raw triad/scope attempt evidence only; advisory runs are read via `read_file(root="runtime_data", path="state/advisory_review.json")` by `snapshot_hash`+`ts`. BIBLE=authority, CHECKLISTS=criteria, Development=procedure; snapshot identity, advisory coverage or audited-skip evidence, deterministic results, actor evidence, and final Git identity must describe the same material.

### Review stack

Review waiting has six independent axes: transport/dead-socket bound, typed active-operation lease, slot/task deadline, budget/cancel, absolute task ceiling, late-result custody. `review_custody.py` is the worker-lifecycle seam used by `review_substrate.py` — no scheduler, no second timing ledger. The active-operation map only spares live physical calls from the idle rail; deadline/budget/cancel/ceiling still win. Delegated-session expiry uses the verified cancel path; API/thread calls disclose `in_flight` and reconcile late answers before the same retry identity re-dispatches.

In a mixed cycle, a settled terminal API error stays in the replayable actor roster (a typed actor state suffices when capture metadata is absent; if present it must say `settled`), so a sibling can't lose the terminal fact or buy a duplicate call. `reserved`/`released` remain retry-eligible, never sticky replay rows; `dispatched`/`unresolved` without a typed terminal HTTP status are custody-lost/no-resend, with such a status retained for same-cycle replay — never a second physical send. Custody is proved by capture/operation state, not the synthetic id: a pre-write-ahead `$0 not_dispatched` row stays frozen/retryable; a post-stamp checkpoint failure cannot rewind paid authority. The retry rail is monotonic: a positive `settled`/`dispatched`/`unresolved` capture outranks a contradictory synthetic `not_dispatched` label; only `reserved`/`released` proves pre-dispatch; `usage_accounting` owns the vocabulary. A later released reservation or budget refusal cannot erase an earlier dispatch; an earlier unknown stays custody-lost/no-resend while a preserved terminal status may replay. A retry token without a durable invocation is custody-lost before route health or project registration on every delegated surface except the separate Skill Review restart contract; a durable token is valid only for its recorded surface/slot/operation — an API row cannot impersonate delegated recovery. A new cycle = a new identity.

Send-time VLM captioning keeps its 90s provider cap; helpers order bounds provider→killable child→ToolEntry minimum via one fixed margin, narrowed inside owner deadline + finalization reserve. Anthropic direct route keeps 120s; neither is a generic review cutoff. Non-delegated Claude advisory child inherits the owner window, 900s process cap without one. A returned response or typed terminal error settles even empty/incomplete (bounded repair may apply); a dead socket after dispatch is `provider_outcome_unknown` and cannot trigger another paid route. Custody never infers pre-dispatch provenance from implicit `__context__`; only explicit `__cause__` or typed transport metadata releases a row. A no-reserve helper uses the raw owner deadline; the round dispatcher passes the reserve explicitly. A spent owner window yields a typed `$0 not_dispatched` row before fan-out; an in-flight triad row stays pending under blocking, never a quorum verdict. A primary call at the deadline boundary enters the local finalization rail, reason `deadline_local` (not "provider outage"); its one zero-reserve grace call stays under the absolute deadline. A reviewed commit has no outer tool cutoff — foreground custody until settlement; inner review/preflight/lock bounds + task/supervisor absolute deadline are the stops.

Retry custody takes an explicit material/cycle identity when supplied: mutable prompt/history is deliberately NOT part of it; a changed snapshot, owner intent, route, or admitted cycle mints a new one (commit review: the staged tree/parent binding). Before either parallel surface starts, one locked write records `paid=True` plus both complete slot rosters + operation ids in the commit-attempt row, unless the owner window already spent its finalization reserve (that roster stays a typed unpaid `$0` wave, no paid stamp). A delegated slot patches only its exact reserved row with `pending_invocation_id` after `START_REQUESTED` and before the provider POST; exact resume preserves rows/tokens; a missing or mismatched operation stays `custody_lost` in every mode. The paid stamp also records the server session+pid owning the process-local reviewer threads; another Agent or respawned sibling is not owner death. Tokenless rows settle as typed infra failure only after the supervisor death seam proves that pid gone (or a later server generation sees it dead); a durable delegated token stays active for exact rejoin; legacy no-owner rows fail closed. This is not a TTL — elapsed time never becomes resend authority. Plan review re-enters an in-flight cycle only when process-local custody can join/replay every dispatched row; the cycle keeps its physical actor set and `$0` health/fit rows, and partial `need_evidence` findings enter the next envelope only after the cycle is terminal. A delegated poll that loses transport after a run id exists preserves the durable invocation — it never cancels a healthy unknown run or creates a second.

- Advisory pre-review (`claude_advisory_review.py`): cheap, staleness-aware; the audited skip covers only advisory admission, never authoritative review, test policy, or snapshot binding.
- Triad (`tools/review.py`): configured slots score the Repo Commit Checklist as JSON; `config.adaptive_quorum` — 2-of-N (N≥3), both (N=2), single (N=1 → loud `single_reviewer_no_diversity` degraded); a configured-≥quorum-but-fewer-responded shortfall is a loud infra quorum failure; same SSOT for scope/plan/skill/acceptance.
- Scope (`tools/scope_review.py`): touched context + Generated Scope Atlas; intent/scope/coupling; fail-closed on unreadable touched files, budget-aware on oversized prompts; blocking follows `OUROBOROS_REVIEW_ENFORCEMENT`.
- Parallel (`tools/parallel_review.py`): two-phase admission — BOTH gate packets (triad api pack + every scope pack) assembled and fit-checked before any dispatch, so a deterministic block dispatches nothing and spends $0 (typed `not_dispatched` placeholders); paid dispatches run concurrently; findings in one round.
- Shared (`review_helpers.py`, `triad_review.py`; dense file/text pack primitives in the `review_pack_files.py` leaf since 6.118.3 with a lazy re-export/shim plane): checklist loading, JSON extraction, usage events, obligations/history scaffolding, actor records.

Task acceptance is a root-owned post-delivery system, separate from the P3 commit gate. `off` disables; `auto`/`required` host-review queued/headless work plus effectful direct work or an explicit typed deliverable/acceptance contract; pure conversation, ordinary read-only research, and child authority produce no root verdict. Before review the supervisor closes subtree admission under the queue lock and requires recursive terminal quiescence; fence ack, subtree lookup, and EWMA timing use the canonical `budget_drive_root`; `state/acceptance_fence_acks/` is not a lifecycle authority. `_run_task_acceptance_review_once` builds one immutable evidence core (verbatim owner directives, deliverable/criteria, subtree statuses, verification/artifact refs, payload provenance, explicit omissions) for the independent panel. Each actor: one substantive call, ≤2 physical attempts (same-route transport retry or extraction-only repair); no acceptance scope actor; `adaptive_quorum` decides. `FAIL` contributes only with the required outcome tier + a bounded correction rail; a bare veto abstains; `DEGRADED` abstains from quorum/obligations (a semantic DEGRADED with a recommendation may feed the advisory capsule); transport/unparseable no-quorum records terminally (`finalized_unaccepted`, `reason=review_degraded`) — never PASS, never revision authority. Clean requires quorum PASS + a `solved` tier + supported evidence per contributing criterion: each 'supported' needs an `evidence_ref` resolving by exact match to the packet's enumerable exhibit keys; a claim id counts only while the host support table shows a passing receipt (unresolvable refs demote the clean bit, disclosed per-actor as `criteria_refs_unresolved`). Gaps exact-dedup into the improvement loop; explicit `max_improvement_passes` binds every policy, otherwise the shared `OUROBOROS_REVIEW_MAX_CYCLES` cap (`improvement passes = cycles − 1`) binds every policy incl. Required+Blocking (no local cap only under `unlimited` or the non-Required+Blocking `until_deadline`-with-deadline alias); deadline/global rails remain. First review reserves ≥200s; later `max(floor, 1.5×EWMA)` from canonical timing events (`alpha=0.5`). The structured axis mirrors to top-level `review_status`. Post-task synthesis recovery runs only at startup from one checkpoint in the canonical `budget_drive_root` result: replays only `pending_once`, terminal-degrades indeterminate `running` without a second paid call; copy-back/artifact finalization owns materialization; a late copy-back may enrich but cannot overwrite a terminal canonical phase. Dissent and blocking obligations stay typed auditable inputs; the root verdict and stop reason are stored separately from the terminal lifecycle/artifact result.

Rationale: diff reviewers catch line-level mistakes, scope catches cross-module contracts; both on one snapshot prevents one hiding the other. Managed exception: both lanes review the same M0→S subject.

Structural smoke gates are a deterministic P3 codebase-size component. `ouroboros/review.py::iter_gated_modules` is the one source inventory (smoke, `codebase_health`, census, UTF-8 gate); the Git candidate is cached + nonignored untracked; exact-ref census injects immutable blobs; module scope is Python everywhere (incl. `tests/`, `devtools/`) + first-party `web/**/*.js`, vendored/minified excluded; the function iterator keeps the narrower runtime scope + exact lexical qualnames.

`ouroboros/size_ratchet_manifest.py` is a generated data-only debt register consumed through AST literals, never executed: exact module debt >1600 lines, `(path, qualname)` function debt >300, the current 1001-1500 band with rationale authority for new/re-entered paths, byte debt >200,000. `review.py::validate_size_ratchet` proves live+staged manifests exact and shrink-only vs the merge-aware committed authority (previous manifest from `HEAD`'s tree, else any parent carrying it; no committed manifest → bootstrap from own tree; no first-parent replay). Enforcement: OFFICIAL CI blocks via the `size_ratchet` pytest lane (tip exactness + `validate_size_ratchet_transition_against_base`, pairwise base→tip); local surfaces (default lanes, `check_worktree_readiness`, `codebase_health`) warn. Two disclosed residuals: pairwise covers only `event.before`/`base.sha`→`HEAD` (growth-and-rollback in one interval uncaptured); the only block is post-push CI, presupposing branch protection/required status checks on `ouroboros` (a repo-settings prerequisite, escalated to the owner). Within a pair debt shrinks but cannot swap, re-enter without authority, grow, or go stale. `scripts/regenerate_size_ratchet.py` refuses an unmerged index, resolves the prior manifest merge-aware, validates the candidate in memory (`validate_size_ratchet_candidate`) before overwriting. `MAX_TOTAL_FUNCTIONS` is the coarse ceiling; raising needs one-line campaign rationale.

A deterministic hot-store growth invariant complements it: `agent_startup_checks.py::hot_store_growth_notes` (surfaced by `context.py::build_health_invariants`, reported once per worker boot as the `hot_store_growth` check in `verify_system_state`) stats `logs/events.jsonl`, `logs/tools.jsonl`, `logs/progress.jsonl`, and `state/usage_attempts.jsonl` against thresholds in `ouroboros/context_budget.py` → WARNING + remediation pointer; sentinel-marked isolated devtool roots suppress (external reader owns the bounded run-local stores).

Prompt-size SSOT: `REVIEW_PROMPT_TOKEN_BUDGET = 920_000` in `ouroboros/tools/review_helpers.py`. `review_context_atlas.py` targets 850K estimated prompt tokens for scope review + deep self-review; the 920K gate stays the hard stop in each caller so oversized behavior cannot drift; plan review builds no Atlas — packets sized per slot by `plan_review_runtime.plan_slot_fit`.

Scope review reserves output headroom inside the reviewer's 1M window: 920K input + `_SCOPE_MAX_TOKENS` (100K) output + tokenizer margin, because provider accounting can exceed the local estimator and 920K+100K > 1M = a hard 400. That physical rejection is UNCONDITIONALLY fail-closed in `max`; no setting turns it into a non-blocking `budget_exceeded` skip. The only control over scope review is context mode: `low` = whole-repository scope review declaredly not performed (typed `skipped_low_context_mode`), `max` = this gate. `scope_review.py` gates INPUT on `_SCOPE_INPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT = min(920K, 1M − _SCOPE_MAX_TOKENS − margin)` (margin currently 155K); the 920K SSOT is untouched.

DENSITY-CALIBRATED: the chars/4 estimator tracks GPT-style tokenizers inside that margin, but Claude-family tokenizers cut code-heavy packs at ~2.5 chars/token, so the ratio is measured, never keyed to a model-name family. `usage_accounting.execute_physical_attempt` records timestamped `(prompt_chars, real prompt_tokens, route_fp)` witnesses after settlement, outside the ledger lock (fail-soft), in the `token_density` namespace of `capability_evidence.json`; density evidence lives ONLY in the canonical data-root store (`capability_evidence.canonical_evidence_root()`) — never a child drive. Cache-bearing usage with unknown prompt semantics records no witness. Main reducer: newest fresh exact-route witness → newest exact-model → neutral 1.0. Review reducer: densest fresh exact-model witness, existing safety factor, never below the 1.65 cold floor. Retention keeps the densest pair + newest bounded remainder; expired witnesses genuinely disappear; equal ticks use a persisted sequence only as a recency tie-breaker (freshness/TTL depend exclusively on `observed_at`); no refreshed running-maximum. `review_helpers.calibrated_input_token_limit` returns the STRICTEST of the 920K cap, `(window − output_reserve) / density`, and the historical absolute-margin form — expiry loosens only within those bounds; provenance reports the branch. `scope_review._effective_scope_input_limit` computes PER CALL (an import-time constant once froze the pre-measurement value); triad (`tools/review.py`), `plan_review.py`, and `deep_self_review.run_deep_self_review` share the helper.

WINDOW-AWARE: a known window from Capability Evidence (`reviewer_window.resolve_reviewer_window`/`reviewer_context_window`/`window_scaled_reserves`; scope keeps its sentinel SIZING policy) replaces the assumed 1M for the input cap; triad/plan/deep size packs per slot's REAL window; sub-1M windows scale output/tokenizer reserves, never zero the slot. UNKNOWN routes keep the FULL-window assumption on those three surfaces (unknown tries Max, never a silent 200K). Only scope review fails CLOSED on absent evidence, because its BLOCKING authority is what a wrong assumption would forge. The seam returns ONE typed `ReviewerWindow` (`window_tokens`/`status`/`stale`/`observed_at`); `blocking_authority_allowed` is a COMPUTED property — `capability_evidence.confirms_at_least(..., require_fresh=True)` — never a model-name side effect. Two forged-verdict routes close: an EXPIRED/outage-carried 1M record (dated evidence read live) and the designated-default sentinel (invented window read sourced). Same-route resolutions serialize on a per-route lock (ONE fetch); the probe is rate-limited by evidence TTL only. A known sub-1M reviewer is advisory-only: in `max` its result is evidence but cannot satisfy the gate; the commit fails CLOSED (no-≥1M case = owner choosing `low` → `skipped_low_context_mode` rows, or an owner-declared RETRIEVING scope slot at ≥200K sourced evidence whose coverage is declared unasserted — never a weaker gate). Same authority if the estimate passes but the provider's REAL tokenizer rejects (`prompt is too long`, `context_length_exceeded`); other provider/transport errors stay fail-closed. Calibration shrinks the PROMPT for the same pinned reviewer — never the model or the ≥1M floor (P3). Plan review fans ONE shared prompt across mixed-family slots, sized PER SLOT: an unfit slot gets a FREE deterministic `preflight_oversize` instead of a guaranteed-400 (`plan_review_runtime.plan_slot_fit`; the slot stays a configured quorum-denominator row). The packet is not tiered (self-modification plans carry BIBLE.md + ARCHITECTURE.md inline), so fewer callable slots than quorum → typed `PLAN_REVIEW_DEGRADED_PREFLIGHT_OVERSIZE`, no reviewer called, naming each cap. Non-responded scope actors surface the provider failure text (`error` in `build_scope_actor_record`) so a deterministic 400 shows in the verdict. The scope coverage contract requires explicit `severity` only on FAIL rows (it decides blocking); PASS rows default `advisory` like the triad parser.

GUARANTEED-FIT: the assembler walks a deterministic degradation ladder instead of skipping.

1. **Full atlas.**
2. **Compact atlas** — the durable `context_manifest` keeps full per-file coverage; the visible prompt keeps a compact path/disposition index.
3. **Required-omit = failure to ASSEMBLE, never a smaller pack** (BIBLE P3): the row records `budget_omitted` (artifact + reason), pack status `required_artifact_omitted`, no consumer reviews the remainder; the ladder shrinks the FIXED part and retries (`budget_exceeded` = even the content-free manifest cannot fit).
4. **Touched files degrade to diff-only** — freely-degradable first, largest-first per tier; an artifact owed in full is reached only after rung 5; degrading one is a typed assembly failure and never buys a fitting pack. Touched TEXT tests (current and deleted) are FREE; binary fixtures and renamed paths stay out; a deleted test over the inline cap keeps its suppressed marker; full post-change snapshots become an explicit `TOUCHED FILE BUDGET DEGRADATION NOTE` while changes stay in the staged diff (captured byte-exact); the ladder DECLARES those paths (`ReviewContextAtlasRequest.diff_only_included`) so the coverage row reads "changes included…; full snapshot omitted". Diff-only is legal ONLY for merely-touched files — an artifact owed in full regardless of the change (`prompts/`, `ouroboros/contracts/`, protected runtime + review stack, canonical docs) declared diff-only is the same `budget_omitted` failure; a required artifact over the 1MB per-file cap fails the same way, never a silent `oversized` row (requiredness computed BEFORE any disposition).
5. **-U0** — drop unchanged hunk context, preserving every file/hunk identity and every `+`/`-` line.

Triad independently applies the same one-pass fit rule (a disclosed touched-path manifest may replace full snapshots duplicated by the complete diff, then the same `-U0`). Every step is a disclosed omission (P1), never silent.

TWO exhausted-ladder terminals, both fail CLOSED: the irreducible prompt (checklist + canonical docs + staged diff) does not fit; or a REQUIRED artifact never assembled. STATUS picks the authority branch (`fixed_overflow` ≥1M, `budget_exceeded` sub-floor); the CAUSE rides `_TouchedContextStatus.unassembled_required`, worded by `_ladder_terminal_cause`; the refusal is also a recorded `atlas_refused` step naming what did not assemble. Both classify through ONE predicate — `review_context_atlas.atlas_assembly_failed` over `ATLAS_ASSEMBLY_FAILURE_STATUSES`. Scope review and deep self-review are strict consumers — no remainder review. Plan review is no longer an Atlas consumer (typed SPEC + agent-declared evidence): an unattachable locator is a named omission; a packet that cannot assemble with the constitutional pack is a typed failure, never a silent reduction. `atlas_unassembled_required` reads the ONE carrier (`manifest["unassembled_required"]`); `ATLAS_MISSING_ARTIFACT_REMEDY` is the strict-consumer remedy. The causes are not exclusive — a mixed state reports BOTH and picks `ATLAS_MIXED_ASSEMBLY_REMEDY` (second cause read with `atlas_hard_budget_overflowed`). For scope, `budget_exceeded`/provider-oversize are recorded as evidence but never satisfy the P3 gate; in `max` they block. The P3 remedy for a structurally oversized repo is shrinking/splitting the tree — never lowering the reviewer below the 1M floor.

In owner-selected `low`, `run_scope_review` returns before assembling anything — the predicate reads `config.get_owner_context_mode()`, never the effective mode; no agent-reachable settings write can author stored Low (only `/api/owner/context-mode`). No reviewer is called, the commit is unscoped, and a typed non-blocking `status="skipped_low_context_mode"` records through the SAME `build_scope_actor_record` surface carrying the fail-closed results — a low-mode commit is never confusable with "scope review silently failed to launch" (P1). Owner policy coupling, not a coverage claim; the one-pass gate keeps returning the normal actor's authoritative/fail-closed status in `max`.

### Planning, deep review, reflection, memory

Plan, acceptance, commit, and deep self-review answer different questions and never inherit one another's authority: planning judges a proposed approach before implementation; acceptance judges the delivered objective; commit authorizes a staged self-change; deep self-review diagnoses the whole system. Reflection and memory persistence learn from execution but approve none of those boundaries.

#### Plan construction and review

`plan_task` reviews an INTENTION before the work starts — the same organ for code, research, a deliverable, or an action in the world. Envelope: goal, plan prose, typed domain-neutral SPEC (`in_scope`, `non_goals`, `acceptance_claims`, `invariants`, `decisions` (choice + rejected alternatives + why), `deferred`, `affected_resources`, `evidence`). `ouroboros/tools/plan_spec.py` normalizes it, mints the only valid `breaks` targets (`goal`, `claim_N`, `invariant_N`, `decision_N`, `deferred_N`), hashes it. Governance docs always come from the system repository; targets/evidence resolve against `active_repo_dir_for(ctx)`; a path escaping the subject, workspace/subject mismatch, or unreadable root is a named omission, never silent.

ONE structural fact tiers the governance pack: `resolve_constitutional` — `constitutional` is true iff a declared `affected_resources`/`evidence` PATH resolves under the system repo (the active binding alone never decides; skill-payload paths under the canonical data root stay exempt). A constitutional plan carries BIBLE.md in full + ARCHITECTURE.md inline for an `api_chat` row (a retrieving `agent_session` row gets the executor's compact form: mandatory full reads at their resolvable locators); assembling without either is a typed failure, never a disclosure. Other plans carry runtime heading-derived nav maps (`context_layout.generate_doc_nav_map`, never a copy) + resolvable pointers. A reviewer's `need_evidence` locator attaches next cycle via the same evidence policy and enters the manifest hash. No plan-kind taxonomy, no `plan_class`, no `context_level`, no scouts, no plan Atlas.

`ouroboros/tools/plan_evidence.py` resolves evidence against exactly two roots (active workspace, system repo) with the shared sensitive-name policy on locator AND target; every refused/missing/truncated/too-large/binary/URL locator is a typed omission (host never fetches URLs). The manifest hash joins spec hash + `constitutional` in the wave fingerprint — changing what reviewers see changes the review identity.

`ouroboros/tools/plan_packet.py` builds the lean packet: task objective (always), spec with ids, plan prose, attached evidence + omissions table, a bounded task-local exploration log, and (cycle 2+) prior findings, dispositions, and the spec delta with the convergence rule. Slots from `reviewer_slot_config`; transport via `review_execution._review_route_executor` — `api_chat` gets the assembled packet in-process; `agent_session` gets the same task as a retrieving reviewer (`host_file_read_attestation: unobserved`). Reviewers return ONLY a typed findings array (`blocking` with a `breaks` id · `note` · `need_evidence` with a locator); the HOST validates membership, demotes invalid-`breaks` blocking to a disclosed note, demotes a repeated `need_evidence` locator to a note held in the aggregate until disposed (dropping it could turn the wave GREEN), keeps failed slots in the quorum denominator, aggregates via `config.adaptive_quorum`. No reviewer emits GREEN as authority; no reviewer writes a competing plan.

`plan_review_state` v2 inside the root task result is the bounded durable authority: each wave records the frozen spec (including `acceptance_claims`, binding acceptance via `contracts/task_contract.effective_acceptance_claims`), spec/evidence hashes, `constitutional`, validated findings, the aggregate, dispositions, paid-ness; recent waves full, older compacted with an explicit omitted count. A paid actor still in flight keeps the wave `DEGRADED` with `review_late_result_pending` even when settled rows meet arithmetic quorum — a late blocking result cannot land after a false GREEN closure. If the owner deadline expires mid-wave, an identical envelope may still run the exact custody reconciliation; it settles the frozen physical set without buying a successor or extending cognition. v1 is read-only; public copies add `legacy_v1_projection` without rewriting. An OPEN v1 wave projects `legacy_open_requires_resubmission`, never auto-closed.

Closure follows the finding class. GREEN closes. REVIEW_REQUIRED (notes/`need_evidence`, or blocking below quorum) closes notes+`need_evidence` via a disposition-only `plan_task` call naming the fingerprint and covering every finding once — no model call, no cost; a below-quorum blocking finding stays open until the spec changes or a paid delta cycle judges its rejection. REVISE_PLAN can never be closed by disposition: the agent changes the spec (new fingerprint, next paid cycle) or rejects a blocking finding with a rationale riding into that cycle. Paid cycles per task bounded by `OUROBOROS_REVIEW_MAX_CYCLES`; an identical envelope replays the wave free (a recorded DEGRADED wave only under the three replay conditions), with ONE exception: an open wave whose BLOCKING findings all carry valid reject dispositions (REVISE_PLAN, or REVIEW_REQUIRED with a below-quorum blocking finding) has earned its promised delta cycle — the same envelope buys exactly one more paid panel.

A wave is paid iff ≥1 slot was physically dispatched. A dispatched DEGRADED panel (no parseable quorum) pays its cycle, records OPEN with per-slot typed failure facts (code + reset time), and reaches the agent as an honest DEGRADED control outcome with the quorum arithmetic — never a host re-call imperative; only a nothing-dispatched wave of typed `$0` skip rows stays unpaid and never replaces a paid predecessor. Before fan-out the engine captures ONE panel health snapshot via `subagents.route_health` (the single manifest reader) — route-level evidence, never per-credential-profile: positive structural evidence of a spent lane (dated window exhaustion with a future reset, or a typed dead-pool code) → `$0` typed skip row staying in the quorum denominator; unknown health dispatches (fail-open); transient daemon states are never skip evidence. The wave records the material health epoch (`{slot, code, reset_at}`, no observed_at) and the reviewer-roster fingerprint (slot ids, targets, routes, pinned session targets/profiles AND efforts). A recorded open DEGRADED wave replays free only under ALL THREE: an identical envelope, a NON-EMPTY recorded structural epoch still matched by a fresh snapshot, and an unchanged reviewer roster (an effort change is a roster change). An empty-epoch DEGRADED wave (slots died at dispatch, invisible to the pre-fan-out snapshot) re-dispatches a PAID panel on the identical envelope — as does a healed or newly-dead lane or a changed roster; a failed snapshot is transient-unknown and keeps the free replay. When the wave's own typed rows prove the quorum structurally unreachable (configured − window-exhausted < quorum), the wave carries `quorum_unreachable` + earliest reset, and under blocking enforcement the finalization gate RELEASES (review stays open, implementation stays held): the agent may finalize honestly — objective terminalizes `blocked_with_evidence` with typed reason `plan_review_quorum_unreachable` — or wait (one-shot `schedule_followup` on the existing scheduler) or ask the owner; host adds facts only, never an answer template. Under blocking, an open wave otherwise holds implementation; an exhausted cap escalates with `review_cycles_exhausted` and an honest blocked terminal; under advisory the agent may proceed with the wave open — the host emits one typed owner-visible `plan_review_advisory_open` event on recording, plus loud disclosure at finalization. Unavailability, invalid state, budget refusal, deadline rails: typed non-authoritative attempts, never substitutes for GREEN.

#### Deep self-review

`type=deep_self_review` bypasses the ordinary loop, calling `deep_self_review.run_deep_self_review` once with `tools=None`; the caller resolves the dedicated model and its observed route window first. `ReviewContextAtlas` supplies repo coverage; the memory whitelist stays full (identity, scratchpad, registry, WORLD, knowledge index, patterns, improvement backlog). The prompt's omission section is bounded, pointing to the complete persisted coverage manifest. A required Atlas artifact that cannot assemble rebuilds only through the declared compact/final-fit path, else an explicit failure — never a silently incomplete review. The call records normal usage evidence, writes the coverage manifest to `state/deep_self_review_context.json`, the report to `memory/deep_review.md`, and returns it as the result. The model has no tools, cannot mutate the repo, and runs no plan/acceptance/commit reviewers; its report is durable diagnostic memory under BIBLE authority — not implementation or publication authority. Unavailable credentials/model, oversized required context, empty output, transport failure: visible task failures, never a clean review.

#### Post-task reflection

The root post-task checkpoint decides whether an error-bearing or non-trivial run warrants Experience Review. `reflection.generate_reflection` sends the Light route a bounded task goal, trace summary, tool-use profile, concrete errors, structured review evidence, child evidence, and the same frozen non-final cost snapshot used by the task summary. It runs outside the tool loop and records its own usage; failure is logged and never erases the delivered result or changes a verdict.

A reflection lands durably: a non-project root appends the full entry to canonical `logs/task_reflections.jsonl`; a project root appends to its project drive (`projects/<id>/logs/task_reflections.jsonl`) and the canonical log gets only a bounded pointer row (task id, timestamp, project, path) — full project text never enters the canonical log feeding future global context. Project reflections are read back: a project-bound task's context includes a bounded tail of its own file (same limits as canonical, labeled project-owned). The headless mirror drive is never the home (prunable); the Pattern Register update stays on the canonical drive in both cases. Every entry carries task identity, evidence, lessons, backlog candidates, validated memory actions. `MEMORY_ACTIONS_JSON` permits only `scratchpad_append`, `knowledge_write`, `identity_update_candidate`, bounded in count and size; `apply_memory_actions` routes accepted actions through the provenance-preserving APIs. An `identity_update_candidate` goes to the scratchpad for review — never auto-written to `identity.md`. Project-scoped tasks write only project knowledge; scratchpad/identity actions are skipped so local facts cannot contaminate the canonical self. Reflection may propose a future campaign or plan-review backlog item but cannot enqueue, review, commit, or enable one.

Only the root runs full post-task synthesis once. Split non-project work uses the canonical budget drive; project work its project drive, forwarding only the sanitized backlog promotion. Children contribute evidence; there is no second global synthesis. `root_phase_checkpoint` makes the paid phase at-most-once across restart. When synthesis runs blocking, the owner's final answer does not wait for it: after the durable result is stored, the final `send_message` delivers immediately over the live worker→supervisor queue while the buffered-return copy is RETAINED (queue.put is not a delivery receipt); both copies share one `delivery_id`; the supervisor suppresses the second via a bounded (256) in-memory deque backed by the DURABLE registry in `supervisor/terminal_delivery.py` (`state/terminal_deliveries.json`, bounded, atomic) — registered only after a successful send, so a failed live send never suppresses the buffered copy. The same file holds a bounded PENDING outbox — ONE seam for normal/cancel/reap terminal paths: a terminal answer is recorded owed BEFORE it is enqueued and cleared in the same write that marks it delivered, so a crash between settle and send replays it on boot and on the supervisor tick. EVERY non-ephemeral root's final answer enters the outbox at durable-result persistence time (the worker mints the canonical `final:<tid>:<digest>` id, registered cross-process-locked against the canonical data root), regardless of the blocking/nonblocking split. Replays: exponential backoff, bounded; a row exhausting attempts — and equally the oldest owed row evicted by newer registrations — is dropped LOUDLY with full text preserved, a typed `terminal_delivery_exhausted` event (distinct `outbox_capacity` reason for eviction), and a chat notice naming the preserved copy — never silent. Dedupe survives restart; external transports stay at-least-once (disclosed). `task_done` still goes last through the buffered return — early `task_done` would release the queue slot and start child-drive cleanup while post-task runs — so a worker reaped during a hung synthesis has already delivered, and the reaper's idempotent `task_done` against the terminal on-disk result stays the only terminal event. Synthesis receives a sealed final package as mandatory ground truth — the delivered text plus the durable result's own artifact manifest (name/size/existence, same store authority that built the result; no second enumeration) — and its prompts state these facts override failure impressions from the error trace, so a recovered deliverable is described as delivered, not missing.

#### Durable memory and project focus

`context.py` assembles static governance, semi-stable memory, and dynamic task evidence without treating truncation as forgetting. `consolidator.py` replaces eligible dialogue/scratchpad history only with explicit summaries + generation-aware archive/provenance records; unreadable/required/unrepresented material fails or gets a visible omission pointer. Knowledge mutation + index rebuild share one lock; scratchpad block mutation + markdown regeneration share one lock — concurrent Presence and owner turns cannot publish an older projection over a newer write. Knowledge writes retain source metadata; identity changes stay on their dedicated authority path. The final Development context matrix + `context_layout.py` own which reference form is resident.

Ouroboros stays ONE identity across Main, project rooms, and Background Consciousness.
